\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell},

(31)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j+d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)n!\big(\frac{\ell-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}.

(32)

\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
can be derived from
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
by changing the variable
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
to
(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
).

2.2
Divergence of the Seed Integral

The signals
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,\text{NS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
and
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,\text{LS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
and one piece of background
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,\text{(B)}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
converge for arbitrary dimension
d
, while
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
,
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
,
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
are not. For example, consider the following summation in
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
when
j=0
:

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{d}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}(n+1)^{d-4}.

(33)

It can be seen that the sum over
n
is divergent if
d\rightarrow 3
. For
\text{Re}\;d<3
, the sum over
n
can be written as

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(n+1)^{d-4}=\zeta(-d+4).

(34)

Therefore,
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
can be regularized as

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j+d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j+d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n}{n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]+2(n+1)^{d-4}\Bigg)-2\zeta(-d+4)\Bigg].

(35)

The sums over
n
in eq. (
2.2
) are finite and it can be continued to arbitrary dimension
d
. A divergence appears when
d\rightarrow 3
in the Riemann zeta function. The Riemann zeta function can be expanded as a series of
(3-d)
:

\zeta(-d+4)=\frac{1}{3-d}+\gamma_{E}+\mathcal{O}((3-d)),

(36)

with
\gamma_{E}
being Euler’s constant. Then the divergence of
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
is:

\displaystyle\text{Div}\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+1}}{8\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}\frac{2}{3-d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]\Gamma(d+p_{12}+2)}{8\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}r_{2}}{r_{1}+r_{2}}\bigg)^{d+p_{12}+2}\frac{2}{3-d}.

(37)

It should be emphasized that the mass parameter
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
in the above formula is defined in the fixed dimension
d=3
. This means that
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
in the expressions for arbitrary
d
should be expanded as a series of
(3-d)
while making sure the mass
m
is fixed for arbitrary
d
. The same procedure can be taken to
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
and
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
. Consider the contribution of
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
and
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
, the divergence of background is

\displaystyle\text{Div}\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,\text{BG}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=-\frac{1}{(4\pi)^{2}}\frac{2}{(3-d)}\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big]\Gamma(d+p_{12}+2)\bigg(\frac{r_{1}r_{2}}{r_{1}+r_{2}}\bigg)^{d+p_{12}+2}.

(38)

This is nothing but the 4-point correlator generated by a contact interaction. Consider the following Lagrangian of interaction:

\Delta\mathscr{L}=-\frac{1}{24}\delta_{\lambda}a^{d-3-p_{12}}\phi_{c}^{4}.

(39)

The seed integral generated by the interaction is:

\displaystyle\delta\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=

\displaystyle-\delta_{\lambda}\sum_{\mathsf{a}=\pm}\mathsf{a}\mathrm{i}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\mathrm{d}\tau\left(-\tau\right)^{d+1+p_{12}}\text{e}^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{1234}\tau}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\delta_{\lambda}\sum_{\mathsf{a}=\pm}\mathsf{a}\frac{\mathrm{i}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}}{k_{1234}^{d+2+p_{12}}}\text{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}\pi(d+2+p_{12})/2}\Gamma(d+2+p_{12})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\delta_{\lambda}\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big]\Gamma(d+p_{12}+2)\bigg(\frac{r_{1}r_{2}}{r_{1}+r_{2}}\bigg)^{d+p_{12}+2}.

(40)

If the coefficient
\delta_{\lambda}
is

\delta_{\lambda}=\frac{1}{(4\pi)^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},

(41)

then the UV divergence (
38
) of the 1-loop correlator can be subtracted by the local term (
2.2
).

If the MS subtraction is taken to remove the divergence of the background, the expression of the background becomes

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}\big(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)(\ell+1)_{2j+p_{12}+4}}{2^{2j+3}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]r_{1}^{2j+p_{12}+5}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}\big(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)(\ell+1)_{p_{12}+4}}{8\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}r_{1}^{p_{12}+5}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n}{n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)\big(\frac{\ell-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4}{2}+n\big)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]+\frac{2}{n+1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle-2\bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big[\pi\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{p_{12}}{2}\big)\big]}{16\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg)\Bigg],

(42)

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{n,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{3}{2}+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2n,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(\ell+1)_{2j+p_{12}+4}}{2^{2j+2}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)n!\big(\frac{\ell+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+p_{2}+4}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{1}{2}+n\\
2+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]r_{1}^{2j+p_{12}+5}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2},3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(\ell+1)_{p_{12}+4}}{4\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}r_{1}^{p_{12}+5}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\frac{3}{2}+2n}{n!\big(\frac{\ell+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)\big(\frac{\ell+p_{2}+4}{2}+n\big)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{1}{2}+n\\
2+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]+\frac{2}{n+1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle-2\bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big(\frac{\pi p_{12}}{2}\big)}{16\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2},3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg)\Bigg].

(43)

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n,\ell=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}\big(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)(\ell+1)_{2j+p_{12}+4}}{2^{2j+3}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)n!\big(\frac{\ell-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]r_{1}^{2j+p_{12}+5}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}\big(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)(\ell+1)_{p_{12}+4}}{8\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}r_{1}^{p_{12}+5}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n}{n!\big(\frac{\ell-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4}{2}+n\big)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]+\frac{2}{n+1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle-2\bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big[\pi\big(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{p_{12}}{2}\big)\big]}{16\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg)\Bigg].

(44)

Here
\mu_{R}
is introduced as the renormalization scale. With the expressions of signals (
2.1
), (
2.1
) and
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,\text{(B)}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
(
2.1
), the full result of the loop seed integral is computed.

2.3
Summary of the Procedure of Dimensional Regularization

In the previous subsection, the dimensional regularization is completed, and the divergence of
d\rightarrow 3
is subtracted. The main procedure is listed below:

•

Take the asymptotic expansions of the summation which is divergent when
d\rightarrow 3
regarding the summation index.

•

Subtract the asymptotic expansions from the expressions of the correlators and add several Riemann zeta functions related to the asymptotic expansions to make sure the expressions are analytically continued to arbitrary
d
.

•

Take the series expansions of the Riemann zeta function with respect to
(3-d)
and subtract the
(3-d)^{-1}
terms.

3  Applications

In the previous section, the divergent summation (
2.2
) is regularized with the Riemann Zeta function. Several divergent summations in de Sitter spacetime can be regularized in this way. Here are some examples.

Figure 2:
Feynman diagram of 1-loop 2-point correlator with a scalar loop.

3.1
2-point Correlator

The first example is the 1-loop 2-point correlator whose Feynman diagram is Fig.
2
. To get the expression of the 2-point correlator, the double folded limit
(r_{1,2}\rightarrow 1)
is taken to the 4-point correlator
[
152
]
. It is better to use the following expression of the background of the 4-point correlator to take the double folded limit
[
152
]
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1}(u_{1}),r_{2}(u_{2}))=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\sin\big[\frac{\pi}{2}(p_{12}+d)\big]\Gamma(p_{12}+d+2+\ell+j)\big(\ell+p_{2}+\frac{d+3}{2}\big)_{j}}{2^{p_{12}+d+1}\ell!\big(\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+\frac{d}{2}\big)_{j+1}\big(\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+\frac{d}{2}\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times u_{1}^{j+\ell+p_{12}+d+2}\bigg(1-\frac{1}{u_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+d+2+\ell+j)\big(\ell+p_{2}+\frac{d+3}{2}\big)_{j}}{2^{p_{12}+d-1}\ell!\big(\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+\frac{d}{2}\big)_{j+1}\big(\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+\frac{d}{2}\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times u_{1}^{j+\ell+p_{12}+d+2}\bigg(1-\frac{1}{u_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)),

(45)

where
u_{1,2}\equiv 2r_{1,2}/(1+r_{1,2})
. Then
u_{2}\rightarrow 1
is taken to get the seed integral which can be used to get the expressions of 3-point correlators
[
152
]
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r(u),1)=

\displaystyle 2^{-p_{12}-d+1}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big[p_{12}+d+2,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+\tfrac{d}{2},-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+\tfrac{d}{2}\big]

\displaystyle\times u^{p_{12}+d+2}{}_{3}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}p_{12}+d+2,p_{2}+\tfrac{d+3}{2},1\\
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+2+\tfrac{d}{2},-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+2+\tfrac{d}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|u\right]+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(46)

The expression of nonlocal and local signals diverge separately. However, the sum of the two signals converge when
r_{2}\rightarrow 1
. To prove this, it is better to change the variable of signals (
15
), (
16
) from
r_{i}
to
u_{i}
, with the help of (
164
):

\displaystyle r^{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathbf{F}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p}(r)\equiv

\displaystyle r^{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}(2r)^{p+d/2+1}\times{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{d}{4}+\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{p}{2}+\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2},\tfrac{d}{4}+1+\tfrac{p}{2}+\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{2}\\
1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|r^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\bigg(\frac{u}{2}\bigg)^{p+d/2+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}p+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|u\right].

(47)

Then by using (
A
), one may get the following formula:

\lim_{r\rightarrow 1}\big(r^{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathbf{F}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p}(r)-r^{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathbf{F}_{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p}(r)\big)=-2^{1-p-d/2}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}p+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
p+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg].

(48)

Therefore, the sum of nonlocal and local signals in the limit
u_{2}\rightarrow 1
is

\mathcal{I}_{\text{S},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r,1)=\Bigg(-2^{1-p_{2}-d/2}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}r^{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathbf{F}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}}(r)\Bigg)+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(49)

The double folded limit of the seed integral is quite difficult. It can be proved that the expression of the 2-point seed integral is (See App.
C
)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\Bigg(-2^{-p_{12}-d+1}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big[p_{12}+d+2,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+1+\tfrac{d}{2},\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+\tfrac{d}{2}\big]

\displaystyle\times{}_{3}\mathrm{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0muF\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}p_{12}+d+2,\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1\\
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+2+\tfrac{d}{2},\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+2+\tfrac{d}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]\Bigg)+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(50)

While taking the Källén-Lehmann integral, the integral contour can be closed with a semi-circle in the proper half
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
-plane to avoid poles from (
3.1
). Therefore, only poles from the spectral function (
20
) are considered. The result of the double folded limit of the loop seed integral
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
is broken into three pieces:

\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)+\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)+\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1).

(51)

The expressions of the three pieces are:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\sum_{n,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]\Gamma(p_{12}+d+2+j)\big(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)_{j}}{n!(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+1+2n)_{j+1}(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+2d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg],

(52)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{d}{2}+2n\big)\sin^{2}\big(\frac{\pi d}{2}\big)\Gamma(p_{12}+d+2+j)\big(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{d}{2}+2n\big)_{j}}{n!(d+p_{1}+1+2n)_{j+1}(d+p_{2}+1+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+2d}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}+n,1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\end{matrix}\Bigg],

(53)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\sum_{n,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]\Gamma(p_{12}+d+2+j)\big(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)_{j}}{n!(d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+1+2n)_{j+1}(d+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+2d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\end{matrix}\Bigg].

(54)

It can be seen that the summation over
n
is divergent when
d\rightarrow 3
:

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)}{(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+1+2n)(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+2n)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{2}(n+1)^{d-4}=\frac{1}{2}\zeta(-d+4)\sim\frac{1}{2(3-d)}.

(55)

\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
can be regularized as

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]\Gamma(p_{12}+d+2+j)\big(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)_{j}}{n!(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+1+2n)_{j+1}(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+2d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]\Gamma(p_{12}+d+2)}{2^{p_{12}+2d+1}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)}{(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+1+2n)(d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+2n)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
1+n,\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]-\tfrac{1}{2}(n+1)^{d-4}\Bigg)+\tfrac{1}{2}\zeta(-d+4)\Bigg].

(56)

The divergence of
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
is

\text{Div}\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]\Gamma(d+p_{12}+2)}{2^{p_{12}+d+4}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(57)

The divergences of
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
and
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
can be calculated in the same way. Taking the sum of the divergences, the divergence of the seed integral is found as

\text{Div}\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=-\frac{\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big]\Gamma(d+p_{12}+2)}{2^{p_{12}+d+5}\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(58)

After taking the double folded limit, (
2.2
) becomes

\delta\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=2^{-d-p_{12}-1}\delta_{\lambda}\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big]\Gamma(d+p_{12}+2).

(59)

It is expected that the divergence of
\mathcal{J}_{\sigma\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
can be subtracted by (
59
). After comparing (
58
) and (
59
), it can be found that the coefficient
\delta_{\lambda}
is

\delta_{\lambda}=\frac{1}{(4\pi)^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},

(60)

which is the same as (
41
).

The full result of the loop seed integral is

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+5+j)\big(2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)_{j}}{n!(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)_{j+1}(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+5)}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)}{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]-\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big[\pi\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{p_{12}}{2}\big)\big]}{16\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg)\Bigg],

(61)

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2n,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\Gamma(p_{12}+5+j)\big(2+2n\big)_{j}}{2^{p_{12}+5}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)n!(p_{1}+4+2n)_{j+1}(p_{2}+4+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{1}{2}+n\\
2+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2},3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\Gamma(p_{12}+5)}{2^{p_{12}+5}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}+2n\big)}{(p_{1}+4+2n)(p_{2}+4+2n)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{1}{2}+n\\
1+n,2+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]-\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big(\frac{\pi p_{12}}{2}\big)}{16\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2},3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg)\Bigg],

(62)

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+5+j)\big(2+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)_{j}}{n!(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)_{j+1}(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+5)}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)}{(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
1+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]-\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big[\pi\big(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{p_{12}}{2}\big)\big]}{16\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg)\Bigg].

(63)

It can be proved that the series in (
3.1
), (
3.1
), (
3.1
) are convergent if
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
is real or
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
is complex and
0<\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\leqslant 1
. For
1<\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu<\frac{3}{2}
, the expressions can be written in another way. See App.
H
for more details.

3.2
1-loop 4-point Functions in
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
Model

Consider a model which is slightly difficult. The interaction is

\Delta\mathscr{L}=-\frac{1}{4}a^{d-1-p_{1}}\phi_{c}^{2}g^{\mu\nu}(\nabla_{\mu}\sigma)(\nabla_{\nu}\sigma)-\frac{1}{4}a^{d-1-p_{2}}\phi_{c}^{2}g^{\mu\nu}(\nabla_{\mu}\sigma)(\nabla_{\nu}\sigma).

(64)

In this work, it is called
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model. Before calculating the correlator, note that there is a quadratic term of
\nabla_{\mu}\sigma
in the interaction. Therefore, there is a determinant
\big(\text{Det}[1+\frac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{1}}\phi_{c,+}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c,+}^{2}][1+\frac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{1}}\phi_{c,-}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c,-}^{2}]\big)^{-1/2}
in the path integral of SK formalism. Using the relation
\text{Det}\,\mathscr{A}=\exp\text{Tr}\,\log\mathscr{A}
, the determinant can be recognized as a contribution to the effective Lagrangian
[
182
]
:

\displaystyle\big(\text{Det}[1+\tfrac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{1}}\phi_{c,+}^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c,+}^{2}][1+\tfrac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{1}}\phi_{c,-}^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c,-}^{2}]\big)^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\propto\exp\bigg[-\Omega^{-1}\int\mathrm{d}^{d+1}x\log\Big[1+\tfrac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{1}}\phi_{c,+}^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c,+}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\Omega^{-1}\int\mathrm{d}^{d+1}x\log\Big[1+\tfrac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{1}}\phi_{c,-}^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c,-}^{2}\Big]\bigg],

(65)

where
\Omega^{-1}
can be written as
\delta^{d+1}(y-y)
, which is divergent. However, the delta function is recognized as 0 in dimensional regularization. There is no need to consider the contribution of this term.

One may attempt to apply Källén-Lehmann representation to the bubble of correlators of
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model. However, it leads to an incorrect result. To find out this, correlators of
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model will be calculated by using Källén-Lehmann representation in this subsection.

For the
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model,
s
-channel of 4-point correlator of conformal scalar
\phi_{c}
is

\langle\phi_{c,\mathbf{k}_{1}}\phi_{c,\mathbf{k}_{2}}\phi_{c,\mathbf{k}_{3}}\phi_{c,\mathbf{k}_{4}}\rangle_{s}=(2\pi)^{d}\delta^{(d)}(\mathbf{k}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}+\mathbf{k}_{3}+\mathbf{k}_{4})\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4}),

(66)

where the loop amplitude
\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}
is

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathsf{ab}\int_{-\infty}^{\tau_{f}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{d+1}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\tau_{2}}{(-\tau_{2})^{d+1}}

\displaystyle\times C_{\mathsf{a}}(k_{1},\tau_{1})C_{\mathsf{a}}(k_{2},\tau_{1})C_{\mathsf{b}}(k_{3},\tau_{2})C_{\mathsf{b}}(k_{4},\tau_{2})\mathcal{Q}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{ab}}\big(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big).

(67)

The loop momentum integral
\mathcal{Q}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{ab}}
is

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\mathsf{ab}}\big(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)\equiv

\displaystyle\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{d}\mathbf{q}}{(2\pi)^{d}}\big[q_{i}q_{i^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{ab}}\big(q;\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)(k_{s}-q)_{i}(k_{s}-q)_{i^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{ab}}\big(|\mathbf{k}_{s}-\mathbf{q}|;\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\partial_{\tau_{1}}\partial_{\tau_{2}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{ab}}\big(q;\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)\partial_{\tau_{1}}\partial_{\tau_{2}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{ab}}\big(|\mathbf{k}_{s}-\mathbf{q}|;\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)\big](-\tau_{1})^{2}(-\tau_{2})^{2}.

(68)

The amplitude
\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}
can be generated from the following seed integral which comes from the interaction (
64
):

\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})\equiv-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathsf{ab}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\mathrm{d}\tau_{1}\mathrm{d}\tau_{2}(-\tau_{1})^{p_{1}}(-\tau_{2})^{p_{2}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{12}\tau_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{34}\tau_{2}}\mathcal{Q}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\mathsf{ab}}\big(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big),

(69)

in this way

\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}=\frac{(-\tau_{f})^{2(d-1)}}{16k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{4}k_{s}^{d-2}}\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{-2,-2}(r_{1},r_{2}).

(70)

The spectral function of Källén-Lehmann representation in
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model is

\rho_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=\frac{\Gamma\big(\frac{d+1}{2}\big)\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\sinh(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})}{2^{8-d}\pi^{\frac{d+7}{2}}\Gamma(d)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)}\big(\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}+2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}\big)^{2}\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg).

(71)

The detail of the calculation is in App.
E
. Someone may expect that

\mathcal{Q}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\mathsf{ab}}\big(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\rho_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\mathsf{ab}}(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}),

(72)

and then

\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\rho_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\mathcal{I}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2}).

(73)

With the expression of the spectral function, it seems that the seed integral of
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model can be computed, in the way which is similar to the method in
\sigma\sigma
model. The expression is

\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\text{NS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})+\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\text{LS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})+\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\text{BG}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2}).

(74)

The signals are

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\text{NS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\frac{(r_{1}r_{2})^{d/2+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\sin[\pi(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{32\pi^{d/2}\Gamma\big(\tfrac{d}{2}\big)\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(1+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big[(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big]^{2}(1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}}\,}{(1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n)_{d-1}}

\displaystyle\times(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n)\mathcal{C}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})\mathbf{F}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{2}}(r_{2})(r_{1}r_{2})^{2n}

\displaystyle\times\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}+2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)^{2}\big]^{2}

\displaystyle+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)),

(75)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\text{LS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{(r_{1}/r_{2})^{d/2+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\sin[\pi(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)]}{32\pi^{d/2}\Gamma\big(\tfrac{d}{2}\big)\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(1+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big[(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big]^{2}(1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}}\,}{(1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n)_{d-1}}

\displaystyle\times(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n)\mathcal{C}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})\mathbf{F}_{-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-d/2-2n,d}^{p_{2}}(r_{2})\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{2n}

\displaystyle\times\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}+2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)^{2}\big]^{2}

\displaystyle+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(76)

The background can be divided into several terms:

\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,\text{BG}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}+\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}+\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}+\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}.

(77)

The label (B) meansm the term is from the poles of Set B listed in Sec.
2.1
of the nonlocal and local tree integrals, while the labels (3A), (3B) and (3C) mean these terms come from the poles of Set A, B and C of the background tree integral. It can be proved that
\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
converges when
d\rightarrow 3
, while the other terms diverge. The expressions of these terms are:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\sin\big(\frac{\pi d}{2}\big)}{16\pi^{d/2}\Gamma\big(\tfrac{d}{2}\big)\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\big[(1+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big]^{2}(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}(1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}}\,}{(1+2n)_{d-1}}(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2n)\mathcal{C}_{d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\mathbf{F}_{d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})r_{1}^{2n+d/2}\big(\mathbf{F}_{d/2+2n,d}^{p_{2}}(r_{2})r_{2}^{2n+d/2}-\mathbf{F}_{-d/2-2n,d}^{p_{2}}(r_{2})r_{2}^{-2n-d/2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg(\frac{d^{2}}{4}+2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+\bigg(\frac{d}{2}+2n\bigg)^{2}\bigg)^{2},

(78)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j+d+3}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}+2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)^{2}\big]^{2},

(79)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{d}{2}+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big(\frac{\pi d}{2}\big)(\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j+d+2}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)n!\big(\frac{\ell+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+d+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{1}{2}+n\\
\frac{d+1}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}+2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+2n\big)^{2}\big]^{2},

(80)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=
