\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j+d+3}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}+2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)^{2}\big]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+n,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+\frac{1}{2}\\
\frac{d+1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]}{2^{d-2}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{-\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=3}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{d}{4}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\big)(\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j}n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{16}+\tfrac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{4}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\big)^{2}\big]^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}(\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+1}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{d}{4}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\big)}{n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)}\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{16}+\tfrac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{4}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\big)^{2}\big]^{2}

\displaystyle+(n+1)^{d-3}\sum_{\iota=-1}^{3}\Upsilon_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,d,\text{A},\ell,0,\iota}^{p_{2}}(n+1)^{\iota}\Bigg)-\sum_{\iota=-1}^{3}\Upsilon_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,d,\text{A},\ell,0,\iota}^{p_{2}}\zeta(-d+3-\iota)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+4}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}(\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+3}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{d}{4}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\big)}{n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{2}\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{2}}\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{16}+\tfrac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{4}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\big)^{2}\big]^{2}

\displaystyle-(n+1)^{d-3}\sum_{\iota=-1}^{1}\Upsilon_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,d,\text{A},\ell,1,\iota}^{p_{2}}(n+1)^{\iota}\Bigg)+\sum_{\iota=-1}^{1}\Upsilon_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,d,\text{A},\ell,1,\iota}^{p_{2}}\zeta(-d+3-\iota)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+6}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}(\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+5}\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{d}{4}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\big)}{n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{3}\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{3}}

\displaystyle\times\big[\tfrac{d^{2}}{16}+\tfrac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{2}+\big(\tfrac{d}{4}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+n\big)^{2}\big]^{2}+(n+1)^{d-4}\Bigg)-\zeta(-d+4)\Bigg]\Bigg\}.

(88)

It can be seen that the divergence of
\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3A})}
when
d\rightarrow 3
is:

\displaystyle\text{Div}\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+1}}{\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\bigg(8\bigg(\Big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\Big)^{4}-\tfrac{d^{2}}{2}\Big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\Big)^{2}+\tfrac{d^{4}}{16}\bigg)

\displaystyle+(76+48\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2})\bigg(\Big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\Big)^{2}-\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}\bigg)+189+264\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+80\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4}\bigg)/(128(3-d))\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+3}}{4\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+4}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-\bigg(4\Big[\Big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+2\Big)^{2}+1-\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}\Big]+19+12\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}\bigg)/(8(3-d))\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+5}}{16\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+6}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(89)

After calculating the divergences of
\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3B})}
and
\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3C})}
, the diverge of the loop seed integral is

\displaystyle\text{Div}\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+1}}{2\pi^{2}}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\bigg(8\bigg(\Big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\Big)^{4}-\tfrac{d^{2}}{2}\Big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\Big)^{2}+\tfrac{d^{4}}{16}\bigg)

\displaystyle+(76+48\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2})\bigg(\Big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\Big)^{2}-\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}\bigg)+189+264\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+80\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4}\bigg)/(128(3-d))\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+3}}{8\pi^{2}}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+4}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[-\bigg(4\Big[\Big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+2\Big)^{2}+1-\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}\Big]+19+12\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}\bigg)/(8(3-d))\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}(\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+5}}{32\pi^{2}}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+6}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(90)

Consider the following interaction as the counter term of
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model:

\displaystyle\Delta\mathscr{L}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24}\delta_{4}a^{d+1}\nabla^{2}(a^{-2-p_{1}}\phi_{c}^{2})\nabla^{2}(a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c}^{2})-\frac{1}{24}\delta_{2}a^{d-1-p_{1}}\phi_{c}^{2}\nabla^{2}(a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c}^{2})-\frac{1}{24}\delta_{0}a^{d-3-p_{12}}\phi_{c}^{4}.

(91)

If only the contribution of
s
-channel is considered, the correlator generated by the interaction is:

\displaystyle\delta\mathcal{J}_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{3}\delta_{4}\sum_{\mathsf{a}=\pm}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\mathsf{a}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\frac{\mathrm{d}\tau}{(-\tau)^{d+1}}\big[-\tau^{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}+(d-1)\tau\partial_{\tau}-\tau^{2}k_{s}^{2}\big]\big[(-\tau)^{d+1+p_{1}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{12}\tau}\big]

\displaystyle\times\big[-\tau^{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}+(d-1)\tau\partial_{\tau}-\tau^{2}k_{s}^{2}\big]\big[(-\tau)^{d+1+p_{2}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{34}\tau}\big]

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{6}\delta_{2}\Bigg[\sum_{\mathsf{a}=\pm}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\mathsf{a}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\frac{\mathrm{d}\tau}{(-\tau)^{d+1}}\big[(-\tau)^{d+1+p_{1}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{12}\tau}\big]

\displaystyle\times\big[-\tau^{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}+(d-1)\tau\partial_{\tau}-\tau^{2}k_{s}^{2}\big]\big[(-\tau)^{d+1+p_{2}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{34}\tau}\big]+(k_{12}\leftrightarrow k_{34})\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{3}\delta_{0}\sum_{\mathsf{a}=\pm}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\mathsf{a}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\frac{\mathrm{d}\tau}{(-\tau)^{d+1}}\big[(-\tau)^{d+1+p_{1}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{12}\tau}\big]\big[(-\tau)^{d+1+p_{2}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{34}\tau}\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}2(-1)^{\ell}\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+1}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\tfrac{1}{3}\delta_{4}\big[\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\big)^{4}-\tfrac{d^{2}}{2}\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\big)^{2}+\tfrac{d^{4}}{16}\big]

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{3}\delta_{2}\big[\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+1\big)^{2}-\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}\big]+\tfrac{1}{3}\delta_{0}\Big)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}2(-1)^{\ell}\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+3}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+4}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\tfrac{2}{3}\delta_{4}\big[\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+p_{2}+\ell+2\big)^{2}+1-\tfrac{d^{2}}{4}\big]+\tfrac{1}{3}\delta_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\tfrac{2}{3}\delta_{4}(-1)^{\ell}\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big](\ell+1)_{d+p_{12}+5}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+6}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}.

(92)

To subtract the divergence of the seed integral, the coefficients
\delta_{4},\delta_{2},\delta_{0}
should be

\frac{1}{3}\delta_{4}=\frac{1}{64\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{2}=\frac{19+12\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{128\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{0}=\frac{189+264\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+80\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4}}{512\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(93)

If the flat space limit
H\rightarrow 0
is taken, the coefficients of the three divergent contact terms are:

\frac{1}{3}\delta_{4}=\frac{1}{64\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{2}=\frac{3m^{2}}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{0}=\frac{5m^{4}}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(94)

Here
m
is the mass of the scalar field
\sigma
.

The result is different from (
202
). The reason is that the spectral representation

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\rho_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\pm\pm}(y,y^{\prime})

(95)

is not equivalent to the bubble

g^{\mu\nu}(y)g^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}(y^{\prime})\nabla_{\mu}\nabla_{\mu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\pm\pm}(y,y^{\prime})\nabla_{\nu}\nabla_{\nu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\pm\pm}(y,y^{\prime}).

(96)

The derivative acting in the step funtion
\theta
is crucial for obtaining the correct result. See
[
93
]
for more details. The integral (
95
) is actually the spectral representation of the following formula:

\displaystyle g^{\mu\nu}(y)g^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}(y^{\prime})\nabla_{\mu}\nabla_{\mu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mp\pm}(y,y^{\prime})\nabla_{\nu}\nabla_{\nu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mp\pm}(y,y^{\prime})\theta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle g^{\mu\nu}(y)g^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}(y^{\prime})\nabla_{\mu}\nabla_{\mu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\pm\mp}(y,y^{\prime})\nabla_{\nu}\nabla_{\nu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\pm\mp}(y,y^{\prime})\theta(\tau^{\prime}-\tau).

(97)

The difference between (
96
) and (
3.2
) is

\displaystyle\pm 2\mathrm{i}(-\tau)^{d+3}[\nabla_{0}\nabla_{0^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mp\pm}(y,y^{\prime})\delta^{d+1}(y-y^{\prime})\theta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{0}\nabla_{0^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\pm\mp}(y,y^{\prime})\delta^{d+1}(y-y^{\prime})\theta(\tau^{\prime}-\tau)]

\displaystyle-(-\tau)^{2d+2}[\delta(y-y^{\prime})]^{2}.

(98)

Therefore, a correction should be added to the formula (
73
).

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\sum_{\mathsf{a}=\pm}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\mathrm{d}\tau(-\tau)^{d+3+p_{12}}e^{\mathrm{i}k_{1234}\tau}[2\mathsf{a}\mathrm{i}\nabla_{0}\nabla_{0^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime}=y)\theta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle+2\mathsf{a}\mathrm{i}\nabla_{0}\nabla_{0^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mathsf{a}\bar{\mathsf{a}}}(y,y^{\prime}=y)\theta(\tau^{\prime}-\tau)-(-\tau)^{d-1}\delta(y-y)].

(99)

Delta function
\delta(y,y)
is recognized as
0
in dimensional regularization. To regularize
\nabla_{0}\nabla_{0^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime}=y)
, the expression of
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime})
in
(d+1)
dimension is written:

D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime})=\frac{1}{(4\pi)^{\frac{d+1}{2}}}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
\frac{d+1}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|\frac{1+Z(y,y^{\prime})}{2}\right],

(100)

Here
Z(y,y^{\prime})
is the imbedding distance between
y
and
y^{\prime}
:

Z(y,y^{\prime})=\frac{\tau^{2}+\tau^{\prime 2}-|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{\prime}|^{2}}{2\tau\tau^{\prime}}.

(101)

Then the derivation
\nabla_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime})
is

[g_{\mu\nu^{\prime}}+n_{\mu}n_{\nu^{\prime}}(1-Z)]D^{\prime}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime})+n_{\mu}n_{\nu^{\prime}}(1-Z^{2})D^{\prime\prime}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime}).

(102)

Because
Z(y,y^{\prime}=y)=1
, the above formula is simplified to

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime}=y)=

\displaystyle g_{\mu\nu^{\prime}}D^{\prime}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime}=y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(4\pi)^{\frac{d+1}{2}}}g_{\mu\nu^{\prime}}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1\\
\frac{d+3}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

(103)

The
00
component of
g_{\mu{\nu}^{\prime}}
at
y=y^{\prime}
is
-1/(-\tau)^{2}
. For the hypergeometric function, dimensional regularization gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(4\pi)^{\frac{d+1}{2}}}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1\\
\frac{d+3}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(4\pi)^{\frac{d+1}{2}}}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,-\frac{d+1}{2}\\
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2},-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{16(3-d)}(9+40\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4})-\frac{1}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{64}(9+40\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4})

\displaystyle\times\big[-3+2\gamma_{E}-\log(4\pi)+2\psi\big(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{5}{2}\big)+2\psi\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{5}{2}\big)\big].

(104)

Equation (
168
) is used in the second line. Therefore, the function (
3.2
) gives

\displaystyle-2\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big]\Gamma(d+p_{12}+2)\bigg(\frac{r_{1}r_{2}}{r_{1}+r_{2}}\bigg)^{d+p_{12}+2}

\displaystyle\times\bigg(\frac{1}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{16(3-d)}(9+40\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4})-\frac{1}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{64}(9+40\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4})

\displaystyle\times\big[-3+2\gamma_{E}-2\log(4\pi)+2\psi\big(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{5}{2}\big)+2\psi\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{5}{2}\big)\big]\bigg).

(105)

After adding the contribution of (
3.2
), the coefficients of divergence in the limit
d\rightarrow 3
should be:

\frac{1}{3}\delta_{4}=\frac{1}{64\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{2}=\frac{19+12\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{128\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{0}=\frac{69+152\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}+48\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4}}{256\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(106)

In flat space limit, they are

\frac{1}{3}\delta_{4}=\frac{1}{64\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{2}=\frac{3m^{2}}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{0}=\frac{3m^{4}}{16\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(107)

This agrees with (
202
). It can be seen that
\delta_{2}
and
\delta_{4}
rely on Hubble parameter
H
. This means that the interaction of
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model should contain interaction between
\phi_{c}
and the curvature of spacetime.

The expression of background
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
in
d=3
is quite complicated. It is shown in App.
F
. If the folded limit
r_{2}\rightarrow 1
or double folded limit
r_{1,2}\rightarrow 1
is taken, the seed integral for 3-point correlators or 2-point correlators will be derived.

3.3
1-loop 4-point Functions in
AA
Model

Next, consider the interaction between
\phi_{c}
and a massive vector field
A
(
AA
model):

\Delta\mathscr{L}=-\frac{1}{4}a^{d-1-p_{1}}\phi_{c}^{2}g^{\mu\nu}A_{\mu}A_{\nu}-\frac{1}{4}a^{d-1-p_{2}}\phi_{c}^{2}g^{\mu\nu}A_{\mu}A_{\nu}.

(108)

The mass of the vector field is
m_{A}
and its mass parameter is defined as
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}\equiv\sqrt{m_{A}^{2}-(d/2-1)^{2}}
. The loop seed integral related to the interaction is

\mathcal{J}_{AA,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})\equiv-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathsf{ab}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\mathrm{d}\tau_{1}\mathrm{d}\tau_{2}(-\tau_{1})^{p_{1}}(-\tau_{2})^{p_{2}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{12}\tau_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{34}\tau_{2}}\mathcal{Q}_{AA,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mathsf{ab}}\big(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big).

(109)

In this model, the
s
-channel of 4-point correlator of
\phi_{c}
is

\langle\phi_{c,\mathbf{k}_{1}}\phi_{c,\mathbf{k}_{2}}\phi_{c,\mathbf{k}_{3}}\phi_{c,\mathbf{k}_{4}}\rangle_{s}=(2\pi)^{d}\delta^{(d)}(\mathbf{k}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}+\mathbf{k}_{3}+\mathbf{k}_{4})\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},AA}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4}),

(110)

with the loop amplitude
\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},AA}

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},AA}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathsf{ab}\int_{-\infty}^{\tau_{f}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\tau_{1}}{(-\tau_{1})^{d+1}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\tau_{2}}{(-\tau_{2})^{d+1}}

\displaystyle\times C_{\mathsf{a}}(k_{1},\tau_{1})C_{\mathsf{a}}(k_{2},\tau_{1})C_{\mathsf{b}}(k_{3},\tau_{2})C_{\mathsf{b}}(k_{4},\tau_{2})\mathcal{Q}_{AA,\mathsf{ab}}\big(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big).

(111)

Here the loop momentum integral
\mathcal{Q}_{AA,\mathsf{ab}}
is

\mathcal{Q}_{AA,\mathsf{ab}}\big(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{d}\mathbf{q}}{(2\pi)^{d}}g^{\mu\nu}g^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\mu^{\prime},\mathsf{ab}}\big(\mathbf{q};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\nu\nu^{\prime},\mathsf{ab}}\big(\mathbf{k}_{s}-\mathbf{q};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big).

(112)

The amplitude can be represented as

\mathcal{L}_{\phi_{c},AA}=\frac{(-\tau_{f})^{2(d-1)}}{16k_{1}k_{2}k_{3}k_{4}k_{s}^{d-2}}\mathcal{J}_{AA}^{-2,-2}(r_{1},r_{2}).

(113)

The expressions of bulk-propagator of massive vector field are

\displaystyle D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},-+}(\mathbf{k};\tau_{1},\tau_{2})=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{d}A_{(n),\mu}(\mathbf{k},\tau_{1})A_{(n),\nu^{\prime}}^{*}(\mathbf{k},\tau_{2}),

(114)

\displaystyle D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},+-}(\mathbf{k};\tau_{1},\tau_{2})=

\displaystyle D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},-+}^{*}(k;\tau_{1},\tau_{2}),

(115)

where mode function
A_{(n),\mu}(\mathbf{k},\tau)
is defined in
[
183
]
. Note that
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},\pm\pm}(k;\tau_{1},\tau_{2})
is not equivalent to
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},\mp\pm}(k;\tau_{1},\tau_{2})\theta(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})+D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},\pm\mp}(k;\tau_{1},\tau_{2})\theta(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})
. Instead, the propagator
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},++}(y,y^{\prime})
in the position space is

\displaystyle D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},++}(y,y^{\prime})=

\displaystyle D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},-+}(y,y^{\prime})\theta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})+D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},+-}(y,y^{\prime})\theta(\tau^{\prime}-\tau)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathrm{i}}{m_{A}^{2}}(-\tau)^{d-1}\delta^{0}_{\mu}\delta^{0}_{\nu^{\prime}}\delta(y-y^{\prime}).

(116)

The expression of
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},--}
is the complex conjugate of
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},++}
. In the calculation of this subsection, the local term
\mathrm{i}(-\tau)^{d-1}\delta^{0}_{\mu}\delta^{0}_{\nu^{\prime}}\delta(y-y^{\prime})/m_{A}^{2}
is ignored at first. After computing the spectral integral, the contribution of local term will be added.

The spectral function of Källén-Lehmann representation of
AA
model is (See App.
E
for the detail of the calculation)

\displaystyle\rho^{\mathcal{P},0}_{AA}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=

\displaystyle\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\sinh(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Gamma\big(\frac{d+1}{2}\big)}{2^{12-d}\pi^{\frac{d+7}{2}}\Gamma(d)\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-1\big)^{2}\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-1\big)^{2}\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg)(8d^{3}-11d^{4}+4d^{5}-32d\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}+24d^{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}

\displaystyle+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 4}+64d\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}-80d^{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+32d^{3}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}-64\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+64d\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{4})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\sinh(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Gamma\big(\frac{d+1}{2}\big)}{2^{12-d}\pi^{\frac{d+7}{2}}\Gamma(d)\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-1\big)^{2}\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-1\big)^{2}\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg)f_{AA}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}),

(117)

where

f_{AA}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\equiv f_{AA,4}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 4}+f_{AA,2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}+f_{AA,0},

(118)

with

\displaystyle f_{AA,4}=

\displaystyle 16,

(119)

\displaystyle f_{AA,2}=

\displaystyle-32d+24d^{2}-64\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2},

(120)

\displaystyle f_{AA,0}=

\displaystyle 8d^{3}-11d^{4}+4d^{5}+64d\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}-80d^{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+32d^{3}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+64d\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{4}.

(121)

With the expression of the spectral function, the seed integral becomes

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{AA}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})\equiv

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\pm}\mathsf{ab}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\mathrm{d}\tau_{1}\mathrm{d}\tau_{2}(-\tau_{1})^{p_{1}}(-\tau_{2})^{p_{2}}e^{\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{12}\tau_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mathsf{a}k_{34}\tau_{2}}\mathcal{Q}_{AA,\mathsf{ab}}\big(k_{s};\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\text{local term}.

(122)

The result of the integration can be divided into three parts:

\mathcal{J}_{AA}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=\mathcal{J}_{AA,\text{NS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})+\mathcal{J}_{AA,\text{LS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})+\mathcal{J}_{AA,\text{BG}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2}).

(123)

The nonlocal signal and local signal are

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{AA,\text{NS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\frac{(r_{1}r_{2})^{d/2+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}\sin[\pi(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})]}{512\pi^{d/2}\Gamma\big(\tfrac{d}{2}\big)\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(1+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big[(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big]^{2}(1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}}\,}{(1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+2n)_{d-1}}

\displaystyle\times(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+2n)\mathcal{C}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})\mathbf{F}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{2}}(r_{2})(r_{1}r_{2})^{2n}

\displaystyle\times\frac{f_{AA}(\mathrm{i}d/2-2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+2\mathrm{i}n)}{\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-1\big)^{2}\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-1\big)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\text{c.c.},

(124)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{AA,\text{LS}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{(r_{1}/r_{2})^{d/2+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}\sin[\pi(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})]}{512\pi^{d/2}\Gamma\big(\tfrac{d}{2}\big)\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(1+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big[(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big]^{2}(1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}}\,}{(1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+2n)_{d-1}}

\displaystyle\times(\mbox{$\frac{\,{d}\,}{2}$}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+2n)\mathcal{C}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+d/2+2n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})\mathbf{F}_{-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-d/2-2n,d}^{p_{2}}(r_{2})\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{2n}

\displaystyle\times\frac{f_{AA}(\mathrm{i}d/2-2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+2\mathrm{i}n)}{\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-1\big)^{2}\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}-1\big)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\text{c.c.}.

(125)

The expression of background is

\mathcal{J}_{AA,\text{BG}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=\mathcal{J}_{AA,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}+\mathcal{J}_{AA,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}+\mathcal{J}_{AA,(\text{3B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}+\mathcal{J}_{AA,(\text{3C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}+\text{local term},

(126)

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{AA,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\sin(\mbox{$\frac{\,{\pi d}\,}{2}$})}{256\pi^{d/2}\Gamma\big(\tfrac{d}{2}\big)\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{\big[(1+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}\big]^{2}(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}(1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+n)_{\frac{d}{2}-1}}\,}{(1+2n)_{d-1}}

(136)

If taking the transformation

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{AA,(\text{3A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+2n\big)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}\big)\big](\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j+d+7}\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}\big)n!\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{j+1}\big(\frac{\ell+d-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+p_{2}+1}{2}+n\big)_{j+1}}

The counter term of
AA
model is

\displaystyle\Delta\mathscr{L}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24}\delta_{4}a^{d+1}\nabla^{2}(a^{-2-p_{1}}\phi_{c}^{2})\nabla^{2}(a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c}^{2})-\frac{1}{24}\delta_{2}a^{d-1-p_{1}}\phi_{c}^{2}\nabla^{2}(a^{-2-p_{2}}\phi_{c}^{2})-\frac{1}{24}\delta_{0}a^{d-3-p_{12}}\phi_{c}^{4},

to make sure that the expression of the background converges when

(142)

The result is different from (

) as expected. Next, the local term in (

g^{\mu\nu}g^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\mu^{\prime},\pm\pm}(y,y^{\prime})D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\nu\nu^{\prime},\pm\pm}(y,y^{\prime})

and

\displaystyle g^{\mu\nu}g^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\mu^{\prime},\mp\pm}(y,y^{\prime})D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\nu\nu^{\prime},\mp\pm}(y,y^{\prime})\theta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})

Eq. (

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}k_{s}^{d+2+p_{12}}\sum_{\mathsf{a}=\pm}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\mathrm{d}\tau(-\tau)^{d+3+p_{12}}e^{\mathrm{i}k_{1234}\tau}\bigg[\frac{1}{m_{A}^{2}}2\mathsf{a}\mathrm{i}D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},00^{\prime},\bar{\mathsf{a}}\mathsf{a}}(y,y^{\prime}=y)\theta(\tau-\tau^{\prime})

(146)

Delta function
\delta(y-y)
vanishes in dimensional regularization. The expression of
D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},\mu\nu^{\prime},-+}(y,y^{\prime})
is
[
183
]

D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},-+,\mu\nu^{\prime}}(y,y^{\prime})=\frac{1}{(4\pi)^{(d+1)/2}m_{A}^{2}}K_{\mu\nu^{\prime}}(y,y^{\prime}),

(147)

where

\displaystyle K_{\mu\nu^{\prime}}(y,y^{\prime})=

\displaystyle(dI^{\prime}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}(Z)+ZI^{\prime\prime}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}(Z))(\partial_{\mu}Z)(\partial_{\nu^{\prime}}Z)

\displaystyle+(-dZI^{\prime}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}(Z)+(1-Z^{2})I^{\prime\prime}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}(Z))\partial_{\mu}\partial_{\nu^{\prime}}Z,

(148)

with

I_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}(Z)={}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
\frac{d+1}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|\frac{1+Z}{2}\right].

(149)

The embedding distance
Z
is defined in (
101
). The expression (
147
) is simplified if
Z=1
:

D_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},-+,\mu\nu^{\prime}}(y,y^{\prime}=y)=-\frac{d}{2(4\pi)^{\frac{d+1}{2}}m_{A}^{2}}g_{\mu\nu^{\prime}}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+1,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+1\\
\frac{d+3}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right].

(150)

Then, dimensional regularization gives

\displaystyle-\frac{d}{2(4\pi)^{\frac{d+1}{2}}m_{A}^{2}}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+1,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+1\\
\frac{d+3}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{d}{2(4\pi)^{\frac{d+1}{2}}m_{A}^{2}}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+1,\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+1,-\frac{d+1}{2}\\
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\frac{1}{2},-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{32\pi^{2}m_{A}^{2}}\frac{1}{16(3-d)}(9+40\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{4})+\frac{1}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{64}(9+40\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{4})

\displaystyle\times\big[-5+6\gamma_{E}-6\log(4\pi)+6\psi\big(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\tfrac{5}{2}\big)+6\psi\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\tfrac{5}{2}\big)\big].

(151)

With the above expression, function (
3.3
) is calculated as

\displaystyle-2\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}(d+p_{12})\big]\Gamma(d+p_{12}+2)\bigg(\frac{r_{1}r_{2}}{r_{1}+r_{2}}\bigg)^{d+p_{12}+2}

\displaystyle\times\bigg(-\frac{3}{32\pi^{2}m_{A}^{4}}\frac{1}{16(3-d)}(9+40\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{4})+\frac{1}{32\pi^{2}m_{A}^{4}}\frac{1}{64}(9+40\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+16\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{4})

\displaystyle\times\big[-5+6\gamma_{E}-6\log(4\pi)+6\psi\big(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\tfrac{5}{2}\big)+6\psi\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\tfrac{5}{2}\big)\big]\bigg)+\mathcal{O}(3-d).

(152)

After considering the correction of the diagram from the local term, the coefficients of the three divergent contact terms are:

\frac{1}{3}\delta_{4}=\frac{1}{64\pi^{2}m_{A}^{4}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{2}=\frac{-5+12\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}}{128\pi^{2}m_{A}^{4}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{0}=\frac{3+24\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}+48\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{4}}{256\pi^{2}m_{A}^{4}}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(153)

In the flat space limit, they are:

\frac{1}{3}\delta_{4}=\frac{1}{64\pi^{2}m_{A}^{4}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{2}=\frac{3}{32\pi^{2}m_{A}^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{0}=\frac{3}{16\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{3-d}.

(154)

The result is the same as (
206
).

MS subtraction can be taken to the background, and the expression of background
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{AA,\text{BG}}^{p_{1}p_{2}}
is listed in App.
F
.

4  Conclusions and Outlooks

In this work, the dimensional regularization for the bubble diagrams in de Sitter spacetime is finished. It can be finished for correlators with spin-0 and spin-1 internal lines of arbitrary mass. The key to the dimensional regularization in dS is to extract the sum that results in divergence when
d\rightarrow 3
, and then take the sum into the Riemann zeta function. With this procedure, the expressions of various kinds of correlators, including 4-point and 2-point, are derived with the minimal subtraction as the renormalization condition.

Eqs. (
106
) and (
153
) show that the coefficients of the counter terms of
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model and
AA
model contain the Hubble parameter
H
. Therefore, there exist interactions between
\phi_{c}
or other scalar fields and the curvature of spacetime in these two models. The Hubble parameter
H
comes from the curvature tensor
R^{\mu}_{\ \,\nu\rho\sigma}
, the Ricci tensor
R_{\mu\nu}
, and the Ricci scalar
R
. However, it makes no difference in de Sitter spacetime.

The dimensional regularization method used in this work may be used in other expressions of loop diagrams such as the expression of background of the correlator with one bubble loop in
[
50
]
. It should be expected that the dimensional regularization can also be taken to correlators containing loops with more complicated topologies. However, the complete expressions for such correlators remain to be derived. The expressions of correlators with these loops should be worked out first before regularization can be applied.

This work mainly focuses on correlators with purely bosonic propagators. In some models of correlators in de Sitter spacetime, some internal lines are fermionic propagators. With the expressions of the propagators of fermions
[
184
,
185
]
, the expressions of the correlators of these models of arbitrary spatial dimension may be computed. Then dimensional regularization can be applied to these correlators.

Appendix

Appendix A
Useful Functions

In this appendix, functions used frequently in this work are listed. First, here are the shorthand notations for the products of Euler
\Gamma
functions:

\displaystyle\Gamma\left[z_{1},\cdots,z_{m}\right]\equiv

\displaystyle~\Gamma(z_{1})\cdots\Gamma(z_{m}),

(155)

\displaystyle\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}z_{1},\cdots,z_{m}\\
w_{1},\cdots,w_{n}\end{matrix}\right]\equiv

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\frac{\,{\Gamma(z_{1})\cdots\Gamma(z_{m})}\,}{\Gamma(w_{1})\cdots\Gamma(w_{n})}.

(156)

Asymptotic expansions of the ratio of Gamma functions for
z\rightarrow\infty
are:

\frac{\Gamma(z+a)}{\Gamma(z+b)}\sim z^{a-b}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\bigg(\begin{matrix}a-b\\
k\end{matrix}\bigg)\mathrm{B}^{(a-b+1)}_{k}(a)z^{-k},

(157)

here
\bigg(\begin{matrix}n\\
m\end{matrix}\bigg)
is the binomial coefficient and
\mathrm{B}^{(\ell)}_{n}(x)
is the generalized Bernoulli polynomial which is defined by

\bigg(\frac{t}{e^{t}-1}\bigg)^{\ell}e^{xt}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{B}^{(\ell)}_{n}(x)\frac{t^{n}}{n!}.

(158)

The definition of the pochhammer symbol
(z)_{n}
is

\displaystyle(z)_{n}\equiv\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}z+n\\
z\end{matrix}\right].

(159)

The generalized hypergeometric function is defined by:

\displaystyle{}_{p}\mathrm{F}_{q}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\\
b_{1},\cdots,b_{q}\end{matrix}\middle|z\right]=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\displaystyle\frac{\,{(a_{1})_{n}\cdots(a_{p})_{n}}\,}{(b_{1})_{n}\cdots(b_{q})_{n}}\displaystyle\frac{\,{z^{n}}\,}{n!}.

(160)

Sometimes the regularized hypergeometric function
{}_{p}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{q}
is used:

{}_{p}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{q}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\\
b_{1},\cdots,b_{q}\end{matrix}\middle|z\right]=\frac{1}{\Gamma\left[b_{1},\cdots,b_{q}\right]}{}_{p}\mathrm{F}_{q}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\\
b_{1},\cdots,b_{q}\end{matrix}\middle|z\right].

(161)

The “dressed” hypergeometric function is also used in this work, which is defined below:

{}_{p}\mathcal{F}_{q}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\\
b_{1},\cdots,b_{q}\end{matrix}\middle|z\right]=\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\\
b_{1},\cdots,b_{q}\end{matrix}\right]{}_{p}\mathrm{F}_{q}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\\
b_{1},\cdots,b_{q}\end{matrix}\middle|z\right].

(162)

There are considerable transformations of hypergeometric functions. Here are some transformations which are used in this work
[
186
,
187
]
:

\displaystyle{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}a,b\\
c\end{matrix}\middle|z\right]=

\displaystyle\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}c,c-a-b\\
c-a,c-b\end{matrix}\bigg]z^{-a}{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}a,a-c+1\\
a+b-c+1\end{matrix}\middle|1-\frac{1}{z}\right]

\displaystyle+\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}c,a+b-c\\
a,b\end{matrix}\bigg]z^{a-c}(1-z)^{c-a-b}{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}c-a,1-a\\
c-a-b+1\end{matrix}\middle|1-\frac{1}{z}\right],

(163)

{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}a,b\\
2b\end{matrix}\middle|z\right]=\bigg(1-\frac{1}{2}z\bigg)^{-a}{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{1}{2}a,\frac{1}{2}a+\frac{1}{2}\\
b+\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|\bigg(\frac{z}{2-z}\bigg)^{2}\right],

(164)

\displaystyle{}_{p+1}\mathcal{F}_{p}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},a_{2},\cdots,a_{p+1}\\
b_{1},b_{2},\cdots,b_{p}\end{matrix}\middle|z\right]=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}A^{(p)}_{k}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},a_{2}\\
s+a_{1}+a_{2}+k\end{matrix}\middle|z\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}A^{(p)}_{k}\Bigg(\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}a_{1},a_{2},s+k\\
a_{1}+s+k,a_{2}+s+k\end{matrix}\bigg]z^{-a_{1}}{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{1},-a_{2}-s-k+1\\
-s-k+1\end{matrix}\middle|1-\frac{1}{z}\right]

\displaystyle+\Gamma(-s-k)z^{-a_{2}-s-k}(1-z)^{s+k}{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}a_{2}+s+k,-a_{1}+1\\
s+k+1\end{matrix}\middle|1-\frac{1}{z}\right]\Bigg),

(165)

with

\displaystyle A^{(p)}_{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}=0}^{k}\frac{(b_{p}+b_{p-1}+\cdots+b_{2}-a_{p+1}-a_{p}-\cdots-a_{3}+k_{2})_{k-k_{2}}(b_{1}-a_{3})_{k-k_{2}}}{(k-k_{2})!}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{k_{3}=0}^{k_{2}}\frac{(b_{p}+b_{p-1}+\cdots+b_{3}-a_{p+1}-a_{p}-\cdots-a_{4}+k_{3})_{k_{2}-k_{3}}(b_{2}-a_{4})_{k_{2}-k_{3}}}{(k_{2}-k_{3})!}

\displaystyle\times\cdots

\displaystyle\times\sum_{k_{p-1}=0}^{k_{p-2}}\frac{(b_{p}+b_{p-1}-a_{p+1}-a_{p}+k_{p-1})_{k_{p-2}-k_{p-1}}(b_{p-2}-a_{p})_{k_{p-2}-k_{p-1}}}{(k_{p-2}-k_{p-1})!}

\displaystyle\times\frac{(b_{p}-a_{p+1})_{k_{p-1}}(b_{p-1}-a_{p+1})_{k_{p-1}}}{k_{p-1}!},

(166)

A^{(2)}_{k}=\frac{(b_{2}-a_{3})_{k}(b_{1}-a_{3})_{k}}{k!}.

(167)

The hypergeometric functions
{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}
with argument unity can be simplified as

{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}a,b\\
c\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}c,c-a-b\\
c-a,c-b\end{matrix}\bigg]

(168)

if
\text{Re}(c-a-b)>0
.

Riemann Zeta function is used in this work to take the dimensional regularization. It is defined as:

\zeta(s)=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{n^{s}},

(169)

for
\text{Re}\;s>1
. Then
\zeta(s)
is defined by analytic continuation. The only simple pole in
\mathbb{C}
is
s=1
with residue 1
[
186
]
.

Appendix B
Pole Structure of the Tree Seed Integral

In this section, the pole structure of the tree seed integral will be studied. It can be found that although the pole structure of nonlocal signal, local signal and background are slightly difficult, the pole structure of the full seed integral is simple.

First, consider the nonlocal signal. For the term shown in (
15
) directly, poles in the lower-half
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
-plane are of interest, while for the hidden term, poles in the upper-half
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
-plane are considered. Poles of (
16
) and (
2.1
) are studied in the same way. The considered poles of the nonlocal signal are:

Poles

terms shown directly

hidden terms

Set 1A:

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}n,

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=+\mathrm{i}n,

(170)

In this appendix
n
goes over all nonnegative integers.

The pole structure of the local signal is slightly difficult. The considered poles of (
16
) are:

Poles

terms shown directly

hidden terms

Set 2A:

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}n,

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=+\mathrm{i}n,

(171)

Set 2B:

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n),

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n).

(172)

The poles of Set 1A and Set 2A are second-order, from the factor
\csc^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
. However, the poles of the sum of the nonlocal and local signals at
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mp\mathrm{i}n
are simple poles. Notice that

\displaystyle r^{-n}\mathbf{F}_{-n,d}^{p}(r)=

\displaystyle(2r)^{p+d/2+1}r^{-n}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{d}{4}+\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{p}{2}-\tfrac{n}{2},\tfrac{d}{4}+1+\tfrac{p}{2}-\tfrac{n}{2}\\
1-n\end{matrix}\middle|r^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2r)^{p+d/2+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\ell!}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{d}{4}+\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{p}{2}-\tfrac{n}{2}+\ell,\tfrac{d}{4}+1+\tfrac{p}{2}-\tfrac{n}{2}+\ell\\
1-n+\ell\end{matrix}\Bigg]r^{2\ell-n}.

(173)

The summand vanishes if
\ell<n
due to the
\Gamma(1-n+\ell)
in the denominator. Therefore,

\displaystyle r^{-n}\mathbf{F}_{-n,d}^{p}(r)=

\displaystyle(2r)^{p+d/2+1}\sum_{\ell=n}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\ell!}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{d}{4}+\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{p}{2}-\tfrac{n}{2}+\ell,\tfrac{d}{4}+1+\tfrac{p}{2}-\tfrac{n}{2}+\ell\\
1-n+\ell\end{matrix}\Bigg]r^{2\ell-n}

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{\ell\rightarrow\ell+n}}

\displaystyle(2r)^{p+d/2+1}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(\ell+n)!}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\tfrac{d}{4}+\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{p}{2}+\tfrac{n}{2}+\ell,\tfrac{d}{4}+1+\tfrac{p}{2}+\tfrac{n}{2}+\ell\\
1+\ell\end{matrix}\Bigg]r^{2\ell+n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle r^{n}\mathbf{F}_{n,d}^{p}(r).

(174)

This cancellation makes that the poles of the sum of the nonlocal and local signals at
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mp\mathrm{i}n
simpler. If
\sinh(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
from the spectral function of Källén-Lehmann representation is considered, there is no pole at
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mp\mathrm{i}n
in the integrand of (
13
).

Here are the considered poles of (
2.1
):

Poles

terms shown directly

hidden terms

Set 3A:

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n),

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n).

(175)

To find out the residues of (
2.1
), it is better to rewrite the expression in the following way:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r_{1},r_{2})=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell+1}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)(\ell+1)_{2j+d+p_{12}+1}}{2^{2j-1}\big(\tfrac{\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+\tfrac{d}{4}\big)_{j+1}\big(\tfrac{\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+\tfrac{d}{4}\big)_{j+1}}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\Big(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\Big)^{\ell}+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}{2^{2j-1}(2\ell)!}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2\ell+2j+d+p_{12}+2,\frac{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+\frac{d}{4}+\ell,\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+\frac{d}{4}+\ell\\
\frac{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+3}{2}+\frac{d}{4}+\ell+j,\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+3}{2}+\frac{d}{4}+\ell+j\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell,j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}{2^{2j-1}(2\ell+1)!}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{2\ell+1}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2\ell+2j+d+p_{12}+3,\frac{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}}{2}+1+\frac{d}{4}+\ell,\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}}{2}+1+\frac{d}{4}+\ell\\
\frac{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}}{2}+2+\frac{d}{4}+\ell+j,\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}}{2}+2+\frac{d}{4}+\ell+j\end{matrix}\Bigg]\Bigg)

\displaystyle+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu))

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{\ell+j\rightarrow j}}

\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}{2^{2j-2\ell-1}(2\ell)!}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+d+p_{12}+2,\frac{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+\frac{d}{4}+\ell,\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1}{2}+\frac{d}{4}+\ell\\
\frac{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+3}{2}+\frac{d}{4}+j,\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+3}{2}+\frac{d}{4}+j\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{j}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}{2^{2j-2\ell-1}(2\ell+1)!}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+3}r_{2}^{-2\ell-1}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+d+p_{12}+3,\frac{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}}{2}+1+\frac{d}{4}+\ell,\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}}{2}+1+\frac{d}{4}+\ell\\
\frac{-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}}{2}+2+\frac{d}{4}+j,\frac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}}{2}+2+\frac{d}{4}+j\end{matrix}\Bigg]\Bigg)

\displaystyle+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(176)

If
n
is even, the residues of poles of Set 3A are

\displaystyle\text{Res}\Big[\mathrm{i}\mathcal{I}^{\prime p_{1}p_{2}}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}(r_{1},r_{2})\Big]_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=\max\{0,\frac{n}{2}\}}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\min\{j,\frac{n}{2}\}}\frac{(-1)^{\frac{n}{2}-\ell}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}\big)\big]}{2^{2j-2\ell-2}\big(\frac{n}{2}-\ell\big)!(2\ell)!}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+d+p_{12}+2,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+\ell+1\\
j-\frac{n}{2}+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+j+2\end{matrix}\Bigg].

(177)

The parameter
j
is not smaller than
n/2
, and
n
is not smaller than
0
, therefore,

\displaystyle\text{Res}\Big[\mathrm{i}\mathcal{I}^{\prime p_{1}p_{2}}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}(r_{1},r_{2})\Big]_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=\frac{n}{2}}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\frac{n}{2}}\frac{(-1)^{\frac{n}{2}-\ell}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}\big)\big]}{2^{2j-2\ell-2}\big(\frac{n}{2}-\ell\big)!(2\ell)!}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+d+p_{12}+2,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+\ell+1\\
j-\frac{n}{2}+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+j+2\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{j\rightarrow j+n/2}}

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\frac{n}{2}}\frac{(-1)^{\frac{n}{2}-\ell}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}\big)\big]}{2^{2j-2\ell+n-2}\big(\frac{n}{2}-\ell\big)!(2\ell)!}r_{1}^{2j+n+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+n+d+p_{12}+2,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+\ell+1\\
j+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n+j+2\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{\ell\rightarrow n/2-\ell}}

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\frac{n}{2}}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}\big)\big]}{2^{2j+2\ell-2}\ell!(n-2\ell)!}r_{1}^{2j+n+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-n+2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+n+d+p_{12}+2,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n-\ell+1\\
j+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n+j+2\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\frac{n}{2}}\frac{2^{d+p_{12}+3+n}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\big(-\frac{n}{2}\big)_{\ell}\big(\frac{-n+1}{2}\big)_{\ell}}{\ell!n!}r_{1}^{2j+n+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-n+2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{n+d+p_{12}}{2}+j+1,\frac{n+d+p_{12}+3}{2}+j\\
j+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n+j+2,-p_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-n+\ell\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2^{d+p_{12}+3+n}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}r_{1}^{n+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-n}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{n+d+p_{12}}{2}+1,\frac{n+d+p_{12}+3}{2}\\
p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n+2\end{matrix}\middle|r_{1}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{n!}{}_{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}-\frac{n}{2},\frac{-n+1}{2}\\
-p_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-n\end{matrix}\middle|r_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2^{\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}+2+n}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}r_{1}^{\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1+n}\mathbf{F}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})\frac{1}{n!}{}_{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}-\frac{n}{2},\frac{-n+1}{2}\\
-p_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-n\end{matrix}\middle|r_{2}^{2}\right].

(178)

If
n
is odd, the residues are

\displaystyle\text{Res}\Big[\mathrm{i}\mathcal{I}^{\prime p_{1}p_{2}}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}(r_{1},r_{2})\Big]_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=\max\{0,\frac{n-1}{2}\}}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\min\{j,\frac{n-1}{2}\}}\frac{(-1)^{\frac{n}{2}-\frac{1}{2}-\ell}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}\big)\big]}{2^{2j-2\ell-2}\big(\frac{n-1}{2}-\ell\big)!(2\ell+1)!}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+3}r_{2}^{-2\ell-1}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+d+p_{12}+3,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+\ell+\frac{3}{2}\\
j-\frac{n}{2}+\frac{3}{2},p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+j+\frac{5}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg].

(179)

It is easily to find that
0\leqslant(n-1)/2\leqslant j
, therefore,

\displaystyle\text{Res}\Big[\mathrm{i}\mathcal{I}^{\prime p_{1}p_{2}}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}(r_{1},r_{2})\Big]_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=\frac{n-1}{2}}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\frac{(-1)^{\frac{n}{2}-\frac{1}{2}-\ell}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}\big)\big]}{2^{2j-2\ell-2}\big(\frac{n-1}{2}-\ell\big)!(2\ell+1)!}r_{1}^{2j+d+p_{12}+3}r_{2}^{-2\ell-1}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+d+p_{12}+3,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+\ell+\frac{3}{2}\\
j-\frac{n}{2}+\frac{3}{2},p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+j+\frac{5}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{j\rightarrow j+(n-1)/2}}

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\frac{(-1)^{\frac{n}{2}-\frac{1}{2}-\ell}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}\big)\big]}{2^{2j-2\ell+n-3}\big(\frac{n-1}{2}-\ell\big)!(2\ell+1)!}r_{1}^{2j+n+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-2\ell-1}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+n+d+p_{12}+2,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{n}{2}+\ell+\frac{3}{2}\\
j+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n+j+2\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{\ell\rightarrow(n-1)/2-\ell}}

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}\big)\big]}{2^{2j-2\ell+n-3}\ell!(n-2\ell)!}r_{1}^{2j+n+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-n+2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2j+n+d+p_{12}+2,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n-\ell+1\\
j+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n+j+2\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\frac{n}{2}}\frac{2^{d+p_{12}+3+n}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\big(-\frac{n}{2}\big)_{\ell}\big(\frac{-n+1}{2}\big)_{\ell}}{\ell!n!}r_{1}^{2j+n+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-n+2\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{n+d+p_{12}}{2}+j+1,\frac{n+d+p_{12}+3}{2}+j\\
j+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n+j+2,-p_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-n+\ell\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{d+p_{12}+3+n}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}r_{1}^{n+d+p_{12}+2}r_{2}^{-n}{}_{2}\mathcal{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{n+d+p_{12}}{2}+1,\frac{n+d+p_{12}+3}{2}\\
p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+n+2\end{matrix}\middle|r_{1}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{n!}{}_{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}-\frac{n}{2},\frac{-n+1}{2}\\
-p_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-n\end{matrix}\middle|r_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}+2+n}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}r_{1}^{\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1+n}\mathbf{F}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})\frac{1}{n!}{}_{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}-\frac{n}{2},\frac{-n+1}{2}\\
-p_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-n\end{matrix}\middle|r_{2}^{2}\right].

(180)

Therefore, the residues of poles which are considered are

\displaystyle\text{Res}\Big[\mathrm{i}\mathcal{I}^{\prime p_{1}p_{2}}_{\text{BG},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}(r_{1},r_{2})\Big]_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(-1)^{n}2^{\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}+2+n}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}r_{1}^{\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1+n}\mathbf{F}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})\frac{1}{n!}{}_{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}-\frac{n}{2},\frac{-n+1}{2}\\
-p_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-n\end{matrix}\middle|r_{2}^{2}\right].

(181)

The residues of poles of Set 2B are

\displaystyle\text{Res}\Big[\mathrm{i}\mathcal{I}^{\prime p_{1}p_{2}}_{\text{L},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}(r_{1},r_{2})\Big]_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}=-\mathrm{i}(d/2+p_{2}+1+n)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{n}2^{\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}+2+n}\pi^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}r_{1}^{\frac{d}{2}+p_{2}+1+n}\mathbf{F}_{d/2+p_{2}+1+n,d}^{p_{1}}(r_{1})\frac{1}{n!}{}_{2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\mathrm{F}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}-\frac{n}{2},\frac{-n+1}{2}\\
-p_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-n\end{matrix}\middle|r_{2}^{2}\right].

(182)

The residues of poles of Set 2B and Set 3A can be canceled. Therefore, the poles shown in this section do not appear in the calculation of the integration (
13
).

Appendix C
2-point Correlator at Tree Level

In this appendix, the folded limit
r\rightarrow 1
is taken to the tree seed integral
\mathcal{I}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r,1)
. The expression of the signal of the integral is:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\text{S},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(r(u),1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg(-2^{1-p_{2}-d/2}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}r^{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}\mathbf{F}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}}(r)\Bigg)+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg(-2^{1-p_{12}-d}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2},p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\frac{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\sin\big[\pi\big(p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]}{\sin\big[\pi\big(p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\big)\big]}u^{p_{1}+d/2+1/2}{}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\frac{1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1-\frac{1}{u}\right]

\displaystyle-2^{1-p_{12}-d}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]u^{p_{1}+d/2+1/2}(1-u)^{-p_{1}-d/2-1/2}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\big(p_{1}+\tfrac{d}{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}\big){}_{2}\mathrm{F}_{1}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\frac{1}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
-p_{1}-\frac{d}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1-\frac{1}{u}\right]\Bigg)+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(183)

Function (
A
) is used in the above formula. For the background, by using (
