Take the folded limit, then the seed integral becomes

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\Bigg(-2^{1-p_{12}-d}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\frac{\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\sin\big[\pi\big(p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)\big]}{\sin\big[\pi\big(p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\big)\big]}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2},p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+2^{-p_{12}-d+1}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big[1+p_{2}+\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+p_{2}+\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big]

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\big[p_{12}+d+2,-p_{1}-\tfrac{d}{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\big]

\displaystyle\times{}_{3}\mathrm{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0muF\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-p_{1}-\frac{d}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\\
-p_{1}+p_{2}+1,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]\Bigg)+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(189)

Using the following identity
[
188
]

\displaystyle{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}a,b,e+f-a-b-1\\
e,f\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}e,f,e-a-b,f-a-b\\
e-a,e-b,f-a,f-b\end{matrix}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{a+b-e}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}e,f\\
a,b,e+f-a-b\end{matrix}\Bigg]{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}e-a,e-b,1\\
1+e-a-b,e+f-a-b\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

(190)

with

\displaystyle a=p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle b=-p_{1}-\frac{d}{2}-\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle e=p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2},

\displaystyle f=-p_{1}+p_{2}+1,

(191)

Equation (
C
) becomes

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\Bigg(-2^{-p_{12}-d+1}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma\big[p_{12}+d+2,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+1+\tfrac{d}{2},\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+1+\tfrac{d}{2}\big]

\displaystyle\times{}_{3}\mathrm{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0muF\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}p_{12}+d+2,\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1\\
p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]\Bigg)+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(192)

The expression holds if
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
is real, or
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
is complex and
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\leqslant\frac{1}{2}
. The hypergeometric functions in the expression diverge if
\frac{1}{2}<\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu<\frac{d}{2}
. Consider the following identity:

{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}a,b,c\\
e,f\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}e,f,s\\
a,s+b,s+c\end{matrix}\bigg]{}_{3}\mathrm{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}e-a,f-a,s\\
s+b,s+c\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

(193)

where
s=e+f-a-b-c
. Set

\displaystyle a=1,

\displaystyle b=p_{12}+d+2,

\displaystyle c=\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle e=p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

\displaystyle f=-p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,

(194)

and the expression becomes

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\Bigg(-2^{-p_{12}-d+1}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+d+2)

\displaystyle\times{}_{3}\mathcal{F}_{2}\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,p_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\\
p_{12}+d+\frac{5}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,1+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]\Bigg)+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\rightarrow(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)).

(195)

This expression converges for arbitrary
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
. However, the above expression is not suitable to be one part of the integrand of the Källén-Lehmann integral. It is hard to find the divergence of
d\rightarrow 3
.

Appendix D
One-Loop Bubble Diagrams in Flat Spacetime

In this appendix, the diagrams related to
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model and
AA
model are calculated in flat spacetime with dimension
D=d+1
of spacetime. These diagrams are computed to compare the coefficients of divergences with those in de Sitter spacetime. It is expected that the coefficients in dS should be the same as those in flat spacetime after taking flat space limit
H\rightarrow 0
.

D.1
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma

The diagram related to
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma
model is

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{-[p\cdot(p+k)]^{2}}{(p^{2}+m^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon)[(k+p)^{2}+m^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{[p\cdot(p+k)]^{2}}{[(p+xk)^{2}+m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]^{2}}

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{p\rightarrow p-xk}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{\{(p-xk)\cdot[p+(1-x)k]\}^{2}}{[p^{2}+m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{(p^{2})^{2}+(1-2x)^{2}(p\cdot k)^{2}-2x(1-x)p^{2}k^{2}+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}}{[p^{2}+m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]^{2}}.

(196)

Then take the Wick rotation:

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{(\mathrm{d}^{D}p)_{E}}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{(p^{2})^{2}+(1-2x)^{2}(p\cdot k)^{2}-2x(1-x)p^{2}k^{2}+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}}{[p^{2}+m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{(\mathrm{d}^{D}p)_{E}}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{(p^{2})^{2}+\big[\frac{1}{D}-\big(\frac{4}{D}+2\big)x(1-x)\big]p^{2}k^{2}+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}}{[p^{2}+m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}\kappa\Omega_{D}\kappa^{D-1}}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{\kappa^{4}+\big[\frac{1}{D}-\big(\frac{4}{D}+2\big)x(1-x)\big]\kappa^{2}k^{2}+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}}{[\kappa^{2}+m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{2}},

(197)

with

\Omega_{D}=\frac{2\pi^{\frac{D}{2}}}{\Gamma\big(\frac{D}{2}\big)}.

(198)

Using the following formula:

\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}\kappa\frac{\kappa^{\ell-1}}{(\kappa^{2}+\nu^{2})^{m}}=\nu^{\ell-2m}\frac{\Gamma\big(\frac{\ell}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(m-\frac{\ell}{2}\big)}{2\Gamma(m)},

(199)

the result is

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{-[p\cdot(k+p)]^{2}}{(p^{2}+m^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon)[(k+p)^{2}+m^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\frac{\Omega_{D}}{(2\pi)^{D}}\bigg\{[m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{\frac{D}{2}}\frac{\Gamma\big(\frac{D}{2}+2\big)\Gamma\big(-\frac{D}{2}\big)}{2}+\bigg[\frac{1}{D}-\big(\frac{4}{D}+2\big)x(1-x)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times k^{2}[m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{\frac{D}{2}-1}\frac{\Gamma\big(\frac{D}{2}+1\big)\Gamma\big(1-\frac{D}{2}\big)}{2}

\displaystyle+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}[m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{\frac{D}{2}-2}\frac{\Gamma\big(\frac{D}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(2-\frac{D}{2}\big)}{2}\bigg\}.

(200)

For the limit in
D\rightarrow 4

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{-[p\cdot(k+p)]^{2}}{(p^{2}+m^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon)[(k+p)^{2}+m^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{16\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{4-D}\bigg(\frac{(k^{2})^{2}}{4}+\frac{3k^{2}m^{2}}{2}+3m^{4}\bigg)+\mathcal{O}((4-D)^{0}).

(201)

Therefore, the coefficients in (
91
) should be

\frac{1}{3}\delta_{4}=\frac{1}{64\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{4-D},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{2}=\frac{3m^{2}}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{4-D},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{0}=\frac{3m^{4}}{16\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{4-D}.

(202)

D.2
AA

The expression of the diagram in
AA
model is

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{-\big(g_{\mu\nu}+\frac{p_{\mu}p_{\nu}}{m_{A}^{2}}\big)\big(g^{\mu\nu}+\frac{(p^{\mu}+k^{\mu})(p^{\nu}+k^{\nu})}{m_{A}^{2}}\big)}{(p^{2}+m_{A}^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon)[(k+p)^{2}+m_{A}^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2m_{A}^{4}}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{Dm_{A}^{4}+m_{A}^{2}(2p^{2}+2p\cdot k+k^{2})+(p^{2}+p\cdot k)^{2}}{(p^{2}+m_{A}^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon)[(k+p)^{2}+m_{A}^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2m_{A}^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{Dm_{A}^{4}+m_{A}^{2}(2p^{2}+2p\cdot k+k^{2})+(p^{2}+p\cdot k)^{2}}{[(p+xk)^{2}+m_{A}^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]^{2}}

\displaystyle{\mathrel{\rightarrow}^{p\rightarrow p-xk}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2m_{A}^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{1}{[p^{2}+m^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]^{2}}\big(Dm_{A}^{4}

\displaystyle+m_{A}^{2}\{2(p-xk)\cdot[p+(1-x)k]+k^{2}\}+\{(p-xk)\cdot[p+(1-x)k]\}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2m_{A}^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{1}{[p^{2}+m_{A}^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]^{2}}\big(Dm_{A}^{4}

\displaystyle+m_{A}^{2}\{2p^{2}+[1-2x(1-x)]k^{2}\}+(p^{2})^{2}+(1-2x)^{2}(p\cdot k)^{2}

\displaystyle-2x(1-x)p^{2}k^{2}+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}\big)

(203)

After taking the Wick rotation, the expression becomes

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2m_{A}^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{(\mathrm{d}^{D}p)_{E}}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{1}{[p^{2}+m_{A}^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{2}}\big(Dm_{A}^{4}

\displaystyle+m_{A}^{2}\{2p^{2}+[1-2x(1-x)]k^{2}\}+(p^{2})^{2}+(1-2x)^{2}(p\cdot k)^{2}

\displaystyle-2x(1-x)p^{2}k^{2}+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2m_{A}^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int\frac{(\mathrm{d}^{D}p)_{E}}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{1}{[p^{2}+m_{A}^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{2}}\Big(Dm_{A}^{4}

\displaystyle+m_{A}^{2}\{2p^{2}+[1-2x(1-x)]k^{2}\}+(p^{2})^{2}+\big[\tfrac{1}{D}-\big(\tfrac{4}{D}+2\big)x(1-x)\big]p^{2}k^{2}

\displaystyle+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2m_{A}^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}x\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}\kappa\Omega_{D}\kappa^{D-1}}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{1}{[\kappa^{2}+m_{A}^{2}+x(1-x)k^{2}]^{2}}\Big(Dm_{A}^{4}

\displaystyle+m_{A}^{2}\{2\kappa^{2}+[1-2x(1-x)]k^{2}\}+(\kappa^{2})^{2}+\big[\tfrac{1}{D}-\big(\tfrac{4}{D}+2\big)x(1-x)\big]\kappa^{2}k^{2}

\displaystyle+x^{2}(1-x)^{2}(k^{2})^{2}\Big)

(204)

In the limit
D\rightarrow 4

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}p}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{-\big(g_{\mu\nu}+\frac{p_{\mu}p_{\nu}}{m_{A}^{2}}\big)\big(g^{\mu\nu}+\frac{(p^{\mu}+k^{\mu})(p^{\nu}+k^{\nu})}{m_{A}^{2}}\big)}{(p^{2}+m_{A}^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon)[(k+p)^{2}+m_{A}^{2}-\mathrm{i}\epsilon]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{16\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{4-D}\bigg(\frac{(k^{2})^{2}}{4m_{A}^{4}}+\frac{3k^{2}}{2m_{A}^{2}}+3\bigg)+\mathcal{O}((4-D)^{0})

(205)

The coefficients in (
140
) are

\frac{1}{3}\delta_{4}=\frac{1}{64\pi^{2}m_{A}^{4}}\frac{1}{4-D},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{2}=\frac{3}{32\pi^{2}m_{A}^{2}}\frac{1}{4-D},\quad\frac{1}{3}\delta_{0}=\frac{9}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{4-D}.

(206)

Appendix E
Expressions of Spectral Function

In this appendix, the spectral function
\rho_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
and
\rho_{AA}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
are computed. The computation is finished in embedding space. Therefore, the embedding formalism of dS is introduced at first. Then the inverse formulae of Källén-Lehmann representation are introduced, which are essential to get the expression of the spectral functions. Finally, the explicit expressions of the spectral functions are computed.

E.1
Embedding Formalism

The introduction in this subsection closely follows
[
23
,
189
]
.

A
(d+1)
-dimensional de Sitter spacetime can be considered as a hypersurface in
(d+2)
-dimensional Minkowski spacetime

-Y_{0}^{2}+Y_{1}^{2}+\cdots+Y_{d+1}^{2}=1.

(207)

The parametrization of the Poincaré patch of dS
d+1
is given by:

Y^{0}=-\frac{1-\tau^{2}+\mathbf{y}^{2}}{2\tau},\quad Y^{i}=-\frac{y^{i}}{\tau},\quad Y^{d+1}=-\frac{1+\tau^{2}-\mathbf{y}^{2}}{2\tau}.

(208)

Consider a symmetric traceless tensor
T_{A_{1}\cdots A_{J}}(Y)
in the embedding space. The tensor satisfies the tangential condition:

Y^{A_{1}}T_{A_{1}\cdots A_{J}}(Y)=0.

(209)

The components of a tensor in dS
d+1
are obtained if the projection is taken

T_{\mu_{1}\cdots\mu_{J}}(y)=\frac{\partial{Y^{A_{1}}}}{\partial{y^{\mu_{1}}}}\cdots\frac{\partial{Y^{A_{J}}}}{\partial{y^{\mu_{J}}}}T_{A_{1}\cdots A_{J}}(Y).

(210)

The indices of the tensor can be contracted with a null vector
W^{A}

T(Y,W)=W^{A_{1}}\cdots W^{A_{J}}T_{A_{1}\cdots A_{J}}(Y),

(211)

with
Y\cdot W=0
.

To recover the tensor, a differential operator is introduced:

\displaystyle K_{A}=

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{d-1}{2}\bigg)\bigg[\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W^{A}}}-Y_{A}\bigg(Y\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W}}\bigg)\bigg]+\bigg(W\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W}}\bigg)\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W^{A}}}-Y_{A}\bigg(Y\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W}}\bigg)\bigg(W\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W}}\bigg)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}W_{A}\bigg[\frac{\partial{{}^{2}}}{\partial{W\cdot\partial W}}-\bigg(Y\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W}}\bigg)\bigg(Y\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W}}\bigg)\bigg].

(212)

The differential operator acts on a monomial of
W^{A}
as

K_{A_{1}}\cdots K_{A_{J}}W^{B_{1}}\cdots W^{B_{J}}=J!\bigg(\frac{d-1}{2}\bigg)_{J}\bigg[\frac{1}{J!}\sum_{\pi}G_{A_{\pi_{1}}}^{\ \ B_{1}}\cdots G_{A_{\pi_{J}}}^{\ \ B_{J}}-\text{traces}\bigg]

(213)

with

G_{AB}=\eta_{AB}-Y_{A}Y_{B},

(214)

the sum
\pi
is over all permutations of the indices
A_{j}
, the traces are subtracted using
G_{AB}
.

The embedding differential operator is

\nabla_{A}=\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{Y^{A}}}-Y_{A}\bigg(Y\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{Y}}\bigg)-W_{A}\bigg(Y\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{W}}\bigg).

(215)

E.2
Inversion Formulae

Källén-Lehmann representation of operator
\mathcal{O}^{(J)}
is
[
23
]

\displaystyle G_{\mathcal{O}^{(J)}}(Y_{1},Y_{2};W_{1},W_{2})=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{J}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\rho_{\mathcal{O}^{(J)}}^{\mathcal{P},\ell}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})[(W_{1}\cdot\nabla_{1})(W_{2}\cdot\nabla_{2})]^{J-\ell}G_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\ell}(Y_{1},Y_{2};W_{1},W_{2})

\displaystyle+\cdots.

(216)

Before writing the inversion formulae, it is convenient to introduce the Wick rotation to Euclidean Anti de Sitter space. The coordinates transform as

Y_{1}(\tau_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1})\xrightarrow{\tau_{1}\rightarrow\tau_{1}e^{\mathrm{i}\pi/2}}X_{1}(\tau_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}),\quad Y_{2}(\tau_{2},\mathbf{y}_{2})\xrightarrow{\tau_{2}\rightarrow\tau_{2}e^{-\mathrm{i}\pi/2}}X_{2}(\tau_{2},\mathbf{y}_{2}).

(217)

Wightman functions in dS are changed to harmonic functions in EAdS:

G_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\ell}(Y_{1},Y_{2};W_{1},W_{2})\rightarrow\Gamma[\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu]\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\ell}(X_{1},X_{2};W_{1},W_{2}).

(218)

The inversion formulae of Källén-Lehmann representation is

\rho_{\mathcal{O}^{(J)}}^{\mathcal{P},\ell}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=\frac{1}{\mathcal{N}_{J,\ell}}\int_{X_{1}}\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\ell}(X_{2},X_{1};K_{2},K_{1})[(K_{1}\cdot\nabla_{1})(K_{2}\cdot\nabla_{2})]^{J-\ell}G_{\mathcal{O}^{(J)}}(X_{1},X_{2};W_{1},W_{2}).

(219)

The formula is integrating
X_{1}
over EAdS. The coefficient
\mathcal{N}_{J,\ell}
is

\mathcal{N}_{J,\ell}=\frac{(2\ell+d-2)(\ell+d-3)!\big[\ell!(J-\ell)!\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)_{\ell}(d+2\ell-1)_{J-\ell}\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}+J+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}+J-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]^{2}}{4^{J-\ell}(4\pi)^{\frac{d+1}{2}}(d-2)!\ell!\Gamma\big(\frac{d+1}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}+\ell+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}+\ell-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\prod_{t=0}^{\ell-1}\big[\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+t-1\big)\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+t-1\big)\big]}

(220)

for
d\geqslant 3
.

E.3
Explicit Expressions

Split representation of propagators in EAdS is
[
189
]

\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\ell}(X_{1},X_{2};W_{1},W_{2})=\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{\pi\ell!\big(\frac{d}{2}-1\big)_{\ell}}\int_{P}\Pi_{\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\ell}(X_{1},P;W_{1},D_{Z})\Pi_{\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\ell}(X_{2},P;W_{2},Z),

(221)

where
\Pi_{\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\ell}(X_{1},P;W_{1},D_{Z})
is a bulk-to-boundary propagator in EAdS, in which
P
is a null vector of
(d+2)
-dimensional Minkowski spacetime.
Z
is an auxiliary vector of
P
, while
D_{Z}
is defined as

D_{Z}^{A}=\bigg(\frac{d}{2}-1+Z\cdot\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{Z}}\bigg)\frac{\partial{}}{\partial{Z_{A}}}-\frac{1}{2}Z^{A}\frac{\partial{{}^{2}}}{\partial{Z\cdot\partial Z}}.

(222)

Notation “
\int_{P}
” means

\int_{P}(\cdots)\equiv\frac{2}{\text{Vol GL}(1,\mathbb{R})^{+}}\int_{P^{0}>0}\mathrm{d}^{d+2}P\delta(P^{2})(\cdots).

(223)

The expression of bulk-to-boundary propagators are given by

\Pi_{\Delta,\ell}(X,P;W,Z)=\mathcal{C}_{\Delta,\ell}\frac{((-2P\cdot X)(W\cdot Z)+2(W\cdot P)(Z\cdot X))^{\ell}}{(-2P\cdot X)^{\Delta+J}},

(224)

with

\mathcal{C}_{\Delta,\ell}=\frac{(\ell+\Delta-1)\Gamma(\Delta)}{2\pi^{\frac{d}{2}}(\Delta-1)\Gamma\big(\Delta+1-\frac{d}{2}\big)}.

(225)

E.3.1
\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma

Spectral density
\rho_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
is

\displaystyle\rho_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{N}_{0,0}\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)^{2}}\int_{X_{1}}\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},0}(X_{2},X_{1})\nabla_{1A_{1}}\nabla_{2A_{2}}G_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,0}(X_{1},X_{2})K_{1}^{\ A_{1}}K_{2}^{\ A_{2}}(W_{1}\cdot\nabla_{1})(W_{2}\cdot\nabla_{2})G_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,0}(X_{1},X_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(\Gamma[\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu])^{2}}{\mathcal{N}_{0,0}\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)^{2}}\int_{X_{1}}\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},0}(X_{2},X_{1})\nabla_{1A_{1}}\nabla_{2A_{2}}\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,0}(X_{1},X_{2})

\displaystyle\times K_{1}^{\ A_{1}}K_{2}^{\ A_{2}}(W_{1}\cdot\nabla_{1})(W_{2}\cdot\nabla_{2})\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,0}(X_{1},X_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4}(\Gamma[\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu])^{2}}{\pi^{3}\mathcal{N}_{0,0}\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)^{2}}\int_{X_{1}}\int_{P_{1}}\int_{P_{2}}\int_{P_{3}}\Pi_{\Delta^{\prime},0}(X_{1},P_{3})\Pi_{\bar{\Delta}^{\prime},0}(X_{2},P_{3})\nabla_{1A_{1}}\Pi_{\Delta,0}(X_{1},P_{1})

\displaystyle\times\nabla_{2A_{2}}\Pi_{\bar{\Delta},0}(X_{2},P_{1})K_{1}^{\ A_{1}}(W_{1}\cdot\nabla_{1})\Pi_{\Delta,0}(X_{1},P_{2})K_{2}^{\ A_{2}}(W_{2}\cdot\nabla_{2})\Pi_{\bar{\Delta},0}(X_{2},P_{2}).

(226)

Due to the following integration
[
189
]
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{J!\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)_{J}}\int_{X}\Pi_{\Delta_{2},0}(X,P_{2})\Pi_{\Delta_{3},J}(X,P_{3};K,Z)(W\cdot\nabla)^{J}\Pi_{\Delta_{1},0}(X,P_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle b(\Delta_{1},\Delta_{2},\Delta_{3},J)\frac{[(Z\cdot P_{1})P_{23}-(Z\cdot P_{2})P_{13}]^{J}}{(P_{12})^{\Delta_{123,0}+J/2}(P_{13})^{\Delta_{132,0}+J/2}(P_{23})^{\Delta_{231,0}+J/2}},

(227)

the integration of
X_{1}
can be computed directly:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)}\int_{X_{1}}\Pi_{\Delta^{\prime},0}(X_{1},P_{3})\nabla_{1A_{1}}\Pi_{\Delta,0}(X_{1},P_{1})K_{1}^{\ A_{1}}(W_{1}\cdot\nabla_{1})\Pi_{\Delta,0}(X_{1},P_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\Delta^{\prime},0}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta,0}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta,0}\int_{X_{1}}\frac{1}{(-2P_{3}\cdot X_{1})^{\Delta^{\prime}}}\Bigg[\frac{4\Delta^{2}P_{1}\cdot P_{2}}{(-2P_{1}\cdot X_{1})^{\Delta+1}(-2P_{2}\cdot X_{1})^{\Delta+1}}+\frac{\Delta^{2}}{(-2P_{1}\cdot X_{1})^{\Delta}(-2P_{2}\cdot X_{1})^{\Delta}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Delta^{2}\frac{-2\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta},0}\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta},0}}{\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta}+1,0}\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta}+1,0}}b(\Delta+1,\Delta+1,\Delta^{\prime},0)+b(\Delta,\Delta,\Delta^{\prime},0)}{P_{31}^{\frac{\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}P_{32}^{\frac{\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}P_{12}^{\frac{2\Delta-\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}},

(228)

with the notation
P_{ij}=-2P_{i}\cdot P_{j}
and

\Delta_{ijl,k}\equiv\frac{\Delta_{i}+k_{i}+\Delta_{j}+k_{j}-\Delta_{l}-k_{l}}{2}.

(229)

The constant
b(\Delta_{1},\Delta_{2},\Delta_{3},J)
is

b(\Delta_{1},\Delta_{2},\Delta_{3},J)=\frac{\Gamma\big(\frac{\Delta_{1}+\Delta_{2}+\Delta_{3}-d+J}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{\Delta_{1}+\Delta_{2}-\Delta_{3}+J}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{\Delta_{1}-\Delta_{2}+\Delta_{3}+J}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{-\Delta_{1}+\Delta_{2}+\Delta_{3}+J}{2}\big)}{2^{1-J}\pi^{-\frac{d}{2}}\Gamma(\Delta_{1})\Gamma(\Delta_{2})\Gamma(\Delta_{3}+J)}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{1},0}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{2},0}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{3},J}.

(230)

The integration of
P_{i}
is finished by
[
23
]
with the help of the following formula:

\int_{P_{1}}\int_{P_{2}}\int_{P_{3}}\frac{\prod_{j=1}^{3}\Pi_{\bar{\Delta}_{j}-k_{j},0}(X_{2},P_{j})}{(P_{12})^{\Delta_{123,k}}(P_{13})^{\Delta_{132,k}}(P_{23})^{\Delta_{231,k}}}\equiv\mathcal{I}^{\text{QFT}}_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}),

(231)

with

\mathcal{I}^{\text{QFT}}_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}=\frac{\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}-\Delta_{123,k}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}-\Delta_{132,k}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}-\Delta_{231,k}\big)\Gamma\big(d-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\Delta_{j}+k_{j}}{2}\big)}{8\Gamma(d)\Gamma(1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}-k_{1})\Gamma(1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}-k_{2})\Gamma(1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{3}-k_{3})}.

(232)

In this case, the integration of
P_{i}
is

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)}\int_{P_{1}}\int_{P_{2}}\int_{P_{3}}\frac{\Pi_{\bar{\Delta}^{\prime},0}(X_{2},P_{3})\nabla_{2A_{2}}\Pi_{\bar{\Delta},0}(X_{2},P_{1})K_{2}^{\ A_{2}}(W_{2}\cdot\nabla_{2})\Pi_{\bar{\Delta},0}(X_{2},P_{2})}{P_{31}^{\frac{\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}P_{32}^{\frac{\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}P_{12}^{\frac{2\Delta-\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\bar{\Delta}^{2}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta},0}\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta},0}}{\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta}+1,0}\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta}+1,0}}\mathcal{I}^{\text{QFT}}_{-1,-1,0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)+\bar{\Delta}^{2}\mathcal{I}^{\text{QFT}}_{0,0,0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu).

(233)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\rho_{\nabla\sigma\nabla\sigma}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=

\displaystyle\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4}(\Gamma[\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu])^{2}}{\pi^{3}\mathcal{N}_{0,0}}\bigg(-2\bar{\Delta}^{2}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta},0}\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta},0}}{\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta}+1,0}\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta}+1,0}}\mathcal{I}^{\text{QFT}}_{-1,-1,0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)+\bar{\Delta}^{2}\mathcal{I}^{\text{QFT}}_{0,0,0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\bigg)

\displaystyle\times\Delta^{2}\bigg(-2\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta},0}\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta},0}}{\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta}+1,0}\mathcal{C}_{\bar{\Delta}+1,0}}b(\Delta+1,\Delta+1,\Delta^{\prime},0)+b(\Delta,\Delta,\Delta^{\prime},0)\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{4}(\Gamma[\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu])^{2}}{2^{7}\pi^{3+d}\Gamma(d)\mathcal{N}_{0,0}}\Bigg[\frac{\big(\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big(\frac{d}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)}{2\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)^{2}}-1\Bigg]\Bigg[\frac{\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big(\frac{d}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)}{2\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)^{2}}-1\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\bigg)^{2}\bigg(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\bigg)^{2}\frac{\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\Big(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\Big)}{\big(\Gamma(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\big)^{2}\Gamma(1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Gamma(1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma\big(\frac{d+1}{2}\big)\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\sinh(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})}{2^{8-d}\pi^{\frac{d+7}{2}}\Gamma(d)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)}\bigg(\frac{d^{2}}{4}+2\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}\bigg)^{2}\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\Big(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\Big).

(234)

E.3.2
AA

By definition, the expression of
\rho^{\mathcal{P},0}_{AA}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)
is

\displaystyle\rho^{\mathcal{P},0}_{AA}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{N}_{0,0}\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)^{2}}\int_{X_{1}}\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},0}(X_{2},X_{1})G_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},1}(X_{1},X_{2};K_{1},K_{2})G_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},1}(X_{1},X_{2};W_{1},W_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(\Gamma[\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}])^{2}}{\mathcal{N}_{0,0}\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)^{2}}\int_{X_{1}}\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},0}(X_{2},X_{1})\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},1}(X_{1},X_{2};K_{1},K_{2})\Omega_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},1}(X_{1},X_{2};W_{1},W_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime 2}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{4}(\Gamma[\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}])^{2}}{\pi^{3}\mathcal{N}_{0,0}\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)^{2}\big(\frac{d}{2}-1\big)^{2}}\int_{X_{1}}\int_{P_{1}}\int_{P_{2}}\int_{P_{3}}\Pi_{\Delta^{\prime},0}(X_{1},P_{3})\Pi_{\bar{\Delta}^{\prime},0}(X_{2},P_{3})\Pi_{\Delta_{A},1}(X_{1},P_{1};K_{1},D_{Z_{1}})

\displaystyle\times\Pi_{\bar{\Delta}_{A},1}(X_{2},P_{1};K_{2},Z_{1})\Pi_{\Delta_{A},1}(X_{1},P_{2};W_{1},D_{Z_{2}})\Pi_{\bar{\Delta}_{A},1}(X_{2},P_{2};W_{2},Z_{2}).

(235)

The integration of
X_{1}
is

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)}\int_{X_{1}}\Pi_{\Delta^{\prime},0}(X_{1},P_{3})\Pi_{\Delta_{A},1}(X_{1},P_{1};K_{1},D_{Z_{1}})\Pi_{\Delta_{A},1}(X_{1},P_{2};W_{1},D_{Z_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\Delta^{\prime},0}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},1}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},1}\int_{X_{1}}\frac{1}{(-2P_{3}\cdot X_{1})^{\Delta^{\prime}}(-2P_{1}\cdot X_{1})^{\Delta_{A}+1}(-2P_{2}\cdot X_{1})^{\Delta_{A}+1}}

\displaystyle\times[(-2P_{1}\cdot X_{1})(-2P_{2}\cdot X_{1})(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot D_{Z_{2}})+2(-2P_{1}\cdot X_{1})(D_{Z_{2}}\cdot X_{1})(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot P_{2})

\displaystyle+2(-2P_{2}\cdot X_{1})(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot X_{1})(D_{Z_{2}}\cdot P_{1})+4(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot X_{1})(D_{Z_{2}}\cdot X_{1})(P_{1}\cdot P_{2})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta_{A}^{2}}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},1}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},1}}{\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},0}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},0}}\big[\Delta_{A}^{2}(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot D_{Z_{2}})+\Delta_{A}(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot P_{2})(D_{Z_{2}}\cdot\partial_{P_{2}})+\Delta_{A}(D_{Z_{2}}\cdot P_{1})(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot\partial_{P_{1}})

\displaystyle+(P_{1}\cdot P_{2})(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot\partial_{P_{1}})(D_{Z_{2}}\cdot\partial_{P_{2}})\big]\frac{b(\Delta_{A},\Delta_{A},\Delta^{\prime},0)}{P_{31}^{\frac{\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}P_{32}^{\frac{\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}P_{12}^{\frac{2\Delta_{A}-\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}}.

(236)

Then

is integrated, the result is

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)}\int_{P_{1}}\int_{P_{2}}\int_{P_{3}}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\Delta_{A}^{2}}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},1}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},1}}{\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},0}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},0}}\big[\Delta_{A}^{2}(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot D_{Z_{2}})+\Delta_{A}(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot P_{2})(D_{Z_{2}}\cdot\partial_{P_{2}})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\frac{d-1}{2}\big)}\int_{P_{1}}\int_{P_{2}}\int_{P_{3}}\frac{b(\Delta_{A},\Delta_{A},\Delta^{\prime},0)}{\Delta_{A}^{2}}\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},1}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},1}}{\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},0}\mathcal{C}_{\Delta_{A},0}}\Bigg[\frac{\big(\Delta_{A}^{2}-\Delta_{A}+\frac{1}{2}\Delta^{\prime}\big)(D_{Z_{1}}\cdot D_{Z_{2}})}{P_{31}^{\frac{\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}P_{32}^{\frac{\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}P_{12}^{\frac{2\Delta_{A}-\Delta^{\prime}}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{2-24\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}}{3}+\Big(8\big[\big(p_{2}+\ell+\tfrac{7}{2}\big)^{2}-\tfrac{5}{4}\big]-10+24\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big[\pi\big(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\tfrac{p_{12}}{2}\big)\big]}{16\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\Bigg)+(76+12\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})/3\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{\ell}\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})(\ell+1)_{p_{12}+8}}{256\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})\big(\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\frac{1}{2}\big)^{2}\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\frac{1}{2}\big)^{2}}r_{1}^{d+p_{12}+6}\bigg(\frac{r_{1}}{r_{2}}\bigg)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\frac{3}{4}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+n}{n!\big(\frac{\ell-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+p_{2}+1}{2}-n\big)_{3}\big(\frac{\ell+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+p_{2}+4}{2}+n\big)_{3}}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\frac{3}{2}+n,\frac{3}{2}+n,\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\frac{1}{2}+n\\

\displaystyle+\frac{16}{n+1}\Bigg)-16\Bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big[\pi\big(2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A}+\tfrac{p_{12}}{2}\big)\big]}{16\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A})\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}+2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{A},3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\Bigg)\Bigg].

Appendix G
More on Spectral Functions

In this appendix, the relation between Källén-Lehmann spectral density
[
23
]
and spectral function in

is studied.

The spectral function in
[
172
]
is derived from
\rho_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}(L)
in
[
172
]
by setting
\sigma_{1}=-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\sigma_{2}=-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2},L=-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}
. The expression is

\displaystyle\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=

\displaystyle~\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2(4\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\frac{\cos[\pi(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})]}{\sin(-\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{3-d}{2},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\\
\frac{2-d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle~\times{}_{7}\mathcal{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mbox{$\frac{\,{2-d}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\tfrac{3-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}}{2},\mbox{$\frac{\,{2-d}\,}{2}$},\mbox{$\frac{\,{2-d}\,}{2}$}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\mbox{$\frac{\,{2-d}\,}{2}$}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}+d/2}{2},\tfrac{\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}+d/2}{2}\\
\tfrac{1-d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}}{2},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},1+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},1-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}-3d/2}{2},\tfrac{4+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}-3d/2}{2}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]

\displaystyle~+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})\Bigg\}+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\leftrightarrow\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}),

(291)

The following formula is used:

\displaystyle{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{A}\\
\mathbf{B}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right]=\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{C}\\
\mathbf{D}\end{matrix}\bigg]-\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{E}\\
\mathbf{F}\end{matrix}\bigg]{}_{7}\mathrm{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{G}\\
\mathbf{H}\end{matrix}\middle|1\right],

(292)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}=

\displaystyle~\{a,1+\tfrac{a}{2},b,c,e,f,g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}=

\displaystyle~\{\tfrac{a}{2},1+a-b,1+a-c,1+a-e,1+a-f,1+a-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}=

\displaystyle~\{1+a-c,1+a-e,1+a-f,1+a-g,b+c-a,b+e-a,b+f-a,b+g-a\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}=

\displaystyle~\{1+a,b-a,1+a-e-f,1+a-c-f,1+a-c-e,

\displaystyle~~\,1+a-c-g,1+a-e-g,1+a-f-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}=

\displaystyle~\{1+2b-a,b+c-a,b+e-a,b+f-a,b+g-a,a-b,

\displaystyle~~\,1+a-c,1+a-e,1+a-f,1+a-g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}=

\displaystyle~\{1+b-c,1+b-e,1+b-f,1+b-g,b-a,1+a,c,e,f,g\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}=

\displaystyle~\{2b-a,1+b-\tfrac{a}{2},b,b+c-a,b+e-a,b+f-a,b+g-a\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}=

\displaystyle~\{b-\tfrac{a}{2},1+b-a,1+b-c,1+b-e,1+b-f,1+b-g\}.

(293)

The parameters in the above identity are set as:

\displaystyle a=1-\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},

\displaystyle b=\frac{d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}{2},

\displaystyle c=1-\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},

(294)

\displaystyle e=\frac{d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}{2},

\displaystyle f=1-\frac{d}{2},

\displaystyle g=1-\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}.

(295)

Then
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
can be written as

\displaystyle\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\Bigg[\frac{1}{16\pi^{d/2+3}}\frac{\cos\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\big)\big]\sin\big[\frac{\pi}{2}\big(-\frac{3d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\big)\big]\sin\big[\frac{\pi}{2}\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})\big]}{\sin(-\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]}

\displaystyle\times\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}\big(\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})\big]\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})\big]

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,-1+d\\

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2(4\pi)^{(d+1)/2}}\frac{\cos[\pi(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})]}{\sin(-\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})}\Gamma\left[\begin{matrix}\frac{3-d}{2},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\\
\frac{2-d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\end{matrix}\right]

\displaystyle\times{}_{7}\mathcal{F}_{6}\left[\begin{matrix}-1+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2},\frac{1+d-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}{2},\frac{d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}{2},\frac{d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}{2},-1+d,\frac{d/2-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}{2},\frac{d/2+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}{2}\\

(296)

The difference between
\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})
and

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\frac{1}{16\pi^{d/2+3}}\Bigg[\frac{\sin\big[\frac{\pi}{2}\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})\big]\sin\big[\tfrac{\pi}{2}\big(\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})\big]}{\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]}

\displaystyle\times\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi d}{2}\big)\Big(\cos(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})+\cos\big[\pi\big(-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\big)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,-1+d\\
\frac{d}{2},\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg)-(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Bigg\}+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\leftrightarrow\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\frac{1}{32\pi^{d/2+3}}\frac{\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi d}{2}\big)\Big(\cos(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})-\cos\big[\pi\big(\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\big)\big]\Big)\Big(\cos(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})+\cos\big[\pi\big(-\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\big)\big]\Big)}{\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,-1+d\\
\frac{d}{2},\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg)-(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Bigg\}+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\leftrightarrow\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\frac{1}{32\pi^{d/2+3}}\frac{\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi d}{2}\big)\Big(\cos^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})+2\cos(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1})\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})-\cos^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})+\sin^{2}\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]\Big)}{\sin\big[\pi\big(\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,-1+d\\
\frac{d}{2},\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg)-(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\rightarrow-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Bigg\}+(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\leftrightarrow\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16\pi^{d/2+3}}\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi d}{2}\big)\Big(\sin\big[\pi\big(\tfrac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]-\sin\big[\pi\big(\tfrac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big)\big]\Big)\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,-1+d\\
\frac{d}{2},\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8\pi^{d/2+3}}\sin\big(\tfrac{\pi d}{2}\big)\cos\big(\tfrac{\pi d}{2}\big)\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}2-d,-1+d\\
\frac{d}{2},\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\end{matrix}\Bigg]\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16\pi^{d/2+2}}\sin(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\frac{1}{\Gamma\big[\frac{d}{2},\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big]}\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg).

(297)

Compare the above formula with
\rho_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})

[
23
]

\rho_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\sinh(\pi\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\Gamma\big(\frac{d+1}{2}\big)}{2^{6-d}\pi^{(d+7)/2}\Gamma\big[d,\frac{d}{2}+\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime},\frac{d}{2}-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}\big]}\prod_{\pm,\pm,\pm}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\frac{d}{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}\pm\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2}\bigg),

it can be derived that

\rho_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}^{\mathcal{P},0}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})=\frac{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime}}{2\pi\mathrm{i}}\big(\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}^{\text{dS}}(\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})-\rho_{\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{1},\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{2}}^{\text{dS}}(-\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{\prime})\big).

(299)

In the flat space limit

, the spectral density

appearing in the Källén-Lehmann decomposition for

becomes
[

]

Appendix H
Convergence of Series of 1-loop 2-point Correlators

In this appendix, the convergence of series of 1-loop 2-point correlators is proved. First, consider the double series in the expression of
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
shown in (
3.1
):

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+5+j)\big(2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)_{j}}{n!(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)_{j+1}(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j}.

(301)

Each row-series and each column-series corresponding to
\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}|\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j}|
are convergent. Then consider the ratios
\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j+1}/\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n+1,j}
and
\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n+1,j}/\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j}
:

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j+1}}{\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j}}=

\displaystyle\frac{(p_{12}+5+j)(2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n+j)}{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+5+2n+j)(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+5+2n+j)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\frac{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3+2n)j+\mathcal{O}(j^{0})}{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+5+2n+j)(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+5+2n+j)},

(302)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n+1,j}}{\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{7}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)(2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n+j)_{2}n(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)_{2}}{\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)(2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n)_{2}(n+1)(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+5+2n+j)_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)_{2}\big(\frac{5}{2}+n\big)\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{5}{2}+n\big)\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{5}{2}+n\big)}{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+5+2n+j)_{2}\big(\frac{3}{2}+n\big)\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\big)\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n\big)}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\big)\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\big)}{\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3+n\big)\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n\big)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-128(j+1)n^{12}/\Big(\big(\tfrac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)(2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n)_{2}(n+1)(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+5+2n+j)_{2}

\displaystyle\times(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+5+2n+j)_{2}\big(\tfrac{3}{2}+n\big)\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{1}{2}+n\big)\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{3}{2}+n\big)

\displaystyle\times\big(-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3+n\big)\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n\big)\Big).

(303)

Therefore, for large
j
and
n
, the ratios are

\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j+1}}{\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n+1,j}}\sim 1-\frac{-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3+2n}{j},

(304)

\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n+1,j}}{\mathcal{A}_{\text{A},n,j}}\sim 1-\frac{j+1}{n}.

(305)

According to Raabe’s test for double series
[
190
]
, the double series (
H
) is convergent if
\text{Re}(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3)>1
. The condition satisfies if
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
is real, or
\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu
is complex and
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu<1
. If
\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu=1
, the summation of
n
starts at
n=1
and it is convergent. The divergence which appears for
1<\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu<\frac{3}{2}
is from the divergence of the 2-point correlator of tree level (See App.
C
for details). However, the divergence of the correlator is merely a flaw in the expression. The expression of
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
can be replaced by the following formula:

\displaystyle\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+5+j)\big(2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)_{j}}{n!(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)_{j+1}(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2},-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2},-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2},-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}\\
-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1\end{matrix}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\Gamma(p_{12}+5+j)\big(2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\big)_{j}}{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4)_{j+1}(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4)_{j+1}}

\displaystyle-\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4\\
2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\bigg]j^{2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-3}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\Gamma\bigg[\begin{matrix}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4\\
2-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu\end{matrix}\bigg]\zeta(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+3)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,d}^{p_{1}p_{2}}\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\Gamma(p_{12}+5)}{2^{p_{12}+6}\pi^{2}\sin^{2}(\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)}\Bigg[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)}{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]-\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\gamma_{E}+\log\mu_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\log 4\pi+\psi\big(\tfrac{3}{2}\big)-\frac{\pi\sin\big[\pi\big(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\tfrac{p_{12}}{2}\big)\big]}{16\cos^{2}(2\pi\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)\mathcal{C}_{\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu,3}^{p_{1}p_{2}}}\bigg)\Bigg].

(306)

Next, consider the following series:

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\frac{\big(\frac{3}{2}-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2n\big)}{(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{1}+4+2n)(-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+p_{2}+4+2n)}

\displaystyle\times\Gamma\Bigg[\begin{matrix}\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{3}{2}+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+\frac{1}{2}+n\\
1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+2+n,-2\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n,-\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu+1+n\end{matrix}\Bigg]-\frac{1}{2(n+1)}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{B}_{\text{A},n}.

(307)

For large
n
, the ratio
\mathcal{B}_{\text{A},n+1}/\mathcal{B}_{\text{A},n}
becomes

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{B}_{\text{A},n+1}}{\mathcal{B}_{\text{A},n}}\sim

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{-9-2p_{12}+4\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{8n^{2}}+\frac{101+p_{12}(29-8\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)+2p_{12}^{2}-2p_{1}p_{2}-68\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-4\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{16n^{3}}+\mathcal{O}(n^{-4})\bigg)

\displaystyle\bigg/\bigg(\frac{-9-2p_{12}+4\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu}{8n^{2}}+\frac{65+p_{12}(21-8\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu)+2p_{12}^{2}-2p_{1}p_{2}-52\mathrm{i}\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu-4\mkern 2.0mu\widetilde{\mkern-2.0mu\nu\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu^{2}}{16n^{3}}+\mathcal{O}(n^{-4})\bigg)

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle 1-\frac{2}{n}+\mathcal{O}(n^{-2}).

(308)

According to Raabe’s test, the series (
H
) is convergent. In summary, the expression of
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{A})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
is convergent, or it can be written in a convergent way. The convergence of
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{B})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
and
\mkern 2.0mu\widehat{\mkern-2.0mu\mathcal{J}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 2.0mu_{\sigma\sigma,(\text{C})}^{p_{1}p_{2}}(1,1)
can be proved in the same way.
